            Self::Apache(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
            Self::MySql(generated) => generated.write_new(destination).map_err(mysql_config_error),
            Self::PostgreSql(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
            Self::MariaDb(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
            Self::Redis(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
            Self::Tomcat(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
        }
    }
}

fn mysql_config_error(error: impl fmt::Display) -> CoreError {
    CoreError::Validation {
        subject: "MySQL configuration",
        message: error.to_string(),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
enum ConfigSavePayload {
    Quick(GeneratedQuickConfig),
    Visual(VisualOutput),
}

impl ConfigSavePayload {
    fn suggested_file_name(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Quick(generated) => generated.suggested_file_name(),
            Self::Visual(output) => output.suggested_file_name().to_owned(),
        }
    }

    fn write_new(&self, destination: &std::path::Path) -> CoreResult<PathBuf> {
        match self {
            Self::Quick(generated) => generated.write_new(destination),
            Self::Visual(output) => output.write_new(destination),
        }
    }

    const fn is_directory_bundle(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Quick(generated) if generated.is_directory_bundle())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct ConfigGeneratorModel {
    active: bool,
    activation_generation: u64,
    mode: ConfigGeneratorMode,
    selected_goal_subject: ConfigurationGoalSubject,
    kind: NginxSiteKind,
    nginx_features: BTreeSet<NginxSiteFeature>,
    active_nginx_configuration: NginxConfigurationSelection,
    forbidden_path_scope: NginxForbiddenPathScope,
    apache_kind: ApacheSiteKind,
    apache_features: BTreeSet<ApacheSiteFeature>,
    active_apache_configuration: ApacheConfigurationSelection,
    apache_forbidden_path_scope: ApacheForbiddenPathScope,
    mysql_features: BTreeSet<MySqlConfigFeature>,
    active_mysql_configuration: MySqlConfigurationSelection,
    postgresql_version: PostgreSqlVersion,
    postgresql_features: BTreeSet<PostgreSqlConfigFeature>,
    active_postgresql_configuration: PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection,
    postgresql_wal_compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression,
    mariadb_version: MariaDbVersion,
    mariadb_features: BTreeSet<MariaDbConfigFeature>,
    active_mariadb_configuration: MariaDbConfigurationSelection,
    mariadb_character_profile: MariaDbCharacterProfile,
    redis_version: RedisVersion,
    redis_bind_profile: RedisBindProfile,
    redis_features: BTreeSet<RedisConfigFeature>,
    active_redis_configuration: RedisConfigurationSelection,
    redis_eviction_policy: RedisEvictionPolicy,
    redis_append_fsync: RedisAppendFsync,
    tomcat_version: TomcatVersionFamily,
    tomcat_connector_address: TomcatConnectorAddress,
    tomcat_features: BTreeSet<TomcatConfigFeature>,
    active_tomcat_configuration: TomcatConfigurationSelection,
    generated: Option<GeneratedQuickConfig>,
    visual_document: VisualDocument,
    selected_database_schema: Option<&'static DatabaseConfigSchema>,
    visual_rows: Vec<VisualRow>,
    selected_visual_node: Option<VisualNodeId>,
    selected_schema_parameter: Option<usize>,
    visual_dirty: bool,
    visual_generation: u64,
}

impl ConfigGeneratorModel {
    fn new(generated: GeneratedNginxConfig) -> Self {
        let visual_document = VisualDocument::nginx_competition_skeleton()
            .expect("checked-in Nginx competition skeleton must remain structurally valid");
        let visual_rows = visual_document.rows();
        Self {
            active: false,
            activation_generation: 0,
            mode: ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick,
            selected_goal_subject: ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx),
            kind: NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            nginx_features: default_nginx_features(NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy),
            active_nginx_configuration: NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            forbidden_path_scope: NginxForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants,
            apache_kind: ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            apache_features: default_apache_features(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy),
            active_apache_configuration: ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            apache_forbidden_path_scope: ApacheForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants,
            mysql_features: default_mysql_features(),
            active_mysql_configuration: MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            postgresql_version: PostgreSqlVersion::V18,
            postgresql_features: default_postgresql_features(),
            active_postgresql_configuration: PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            postgresql_wal_compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression::Pglz,
            mariadb_version: MariaDbVersion::V12_3,
            mariadb_features: default_mariadb_features(),
            active_mariadb_configuration: MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            mariadb_character_profile: MariaDbCharacterProfile::UnicodeCaseInsensitive,
            redis_version: RedisVersion::V8_10,
            redis_bind_profile: RedisBindProfile::DualStackLoopback,
            redis_features: default_redis_features(),
            active_redis_configuration: RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            redis_eviction_policy: RedisEvictionPolicy::NoEviction,
            redis_append_fsync: RedisAppendFsync::EverySecond,
            tomcat_version: TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0,
            tomcat_connector_address: TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv4Loopback,
            tomcat_features: default_tomcat_features(),
            active_tomcat_configuration: TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            generated: Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Nginx(generated)),
            visual_document,
            selected_database_schema: None,
            visual_rows,
            selected_visual_node: None,
            selected_schema_parameter: None,
            visual_dirty: false,
            visual_generation: 1,
        }
    }

    fn activate(&mut self) {
        self.active = true;
        self.activation_generation = self.activation_generation.wrapping_add(1).max(1);
    }

    fn deactivate(&mut self) {
        self.active = false;
        self.activation_generation = self.activation_generation.wrapping_add(1).max(1);
    }

    fn accepts(&self, activation_generation: u64) -> bool {
        self.active && self.activation_generation == activation_generation
    }

    fn refresh_visual_rows(&mut self) {
        let schema = self.current_database_schema();
        self.visual_rows = self.visual_document.rows_with_database_schema(schema);
        // Core node ids are revision leases. Never carry either document or
        // catalog selection through a reparse/schema reclassification.
        self.selected_visual_node = None;
        self.selected_schema_parameter = None;
    }

    fn selected_visual_row(&self) -> Option<&VisualRow> {
        let selected = self.selected_visual_node?;
        self.visual_rows.iter().find(|row| row.id() == selected)
    }

    fn selected_schema_parameter(&self) -> Option<&'static DatabaseConfigParameterSchema> {
        let schema = self.current_database_schema()?;
        schema.parameters.get(self.selected_schema_parameter?)
    }

    fn filtered_visual_row_indices(&self, query: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
        filtered_visual_row_indices(&self.visual_rows, self.current_database_schema(), query)
    }

    fn filtered_schema_parameter_indices(&self, query: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
        self.current_database_schema()
            .map(|schema| filtered_schema_parameter_indices(schema, query))
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn reconcile_visual_search_selection(&mut self, query: &str) -> bool {
        let visible_rows = self.filtered_visual_row_indices(query);
        let selected_row_visible = self.selected_visual_node.is_none_or(|selected| {
            self.visual_rows
                .iter()
                .position(|row| row.id() == selected)
                .is_some_and(|index| visible_rows.contains(&index))
        });
        let visible_parameters = self.filtered_schema_parameter_indices(query);
        let selected_parameter_visible = self
            .selected_schema_parameter
            .is_none_or(|index| visible_parameters.contains(&index));
        let cleared = !selected_row_visible || !selected_parameter_visible;
        if !selected_row_visible {
            self.selected_visual_node = None;
        }
        if !selected_parameter_visible {
            self.selected_schema_parameter = None;
        }
        cleared
    }

    fn select_visible_visual_node(&mut self, id: VisualNodeId, query: &str) -> Option<VisualRow> {
        let visible_rows = self.filtered_visual_row_indices(query);
        let (index, row) = self
            .visual_rows
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .find(|(_, row)| row.id() == id)?;
        if !visible_rows.contains(&index) {
            return None;
        }
        let row = row.clone();
        self.selected_visual_node = Some(id);
        self.selected_schema_parameter = None;
        Some(row)
    }

    fn select_visible_schema_parameter(
        &mut self,
        expected_schema: &'static DatabaseConfigSchema,
        index: usize,
        query: &str,
    ) -> Option<&'static DatabaseConfigParameterSchema> {
        if !self
            .current_database_schema()
            .is_some_and(|schema| std::ptr::eq(schema, expected_schema))
        {
            return None;
        }
        let visible_parameters = self.filtered_schema_parameter_indices(query);
        if !visible_parameters.contains(&index) {
            return None;
        }
        let parameter = expected_schema.parameters.get(index)?;
        self.selected_visual_node = None;
        self.selected_schema_parameter = Some(index);
        Some(parameter)
    }

    fn can_mutate_selected_local_draft_row(&self) -> bool {
        self.visual_document.kind().allows_lossless_local_draft()
            && self
                .selected_visual_row()
                .is_some_and(VisualRow::allows_local_draft_mutation)
    }

    fn current_database_schema(&self) -> Option<&'static DatabaseConfigSchema> {
        let VisualDocumentKind::Database(artifact) = self.visual_document.kind() else {
            return None;
        };
        self.selected_database_schema
            .filter(|schema| database_schema_matches_artifact(schema, artifact))
    }

    fn select_database_schema_from_registry(
        &mut self,
        schema: &'static DatabaseConfigSchema,
        registry: &'static [DatabaseConfigSchema],
    ) -> bool {
        let VisualDocumentKind::Database(artifact) = self.visual_document.kind() else {
            return false;
        };
        if !database_schema_matches_artifact(schema, artifact)
            || !registry
                .iter()
                .any(|candidate| std::ptr::eq(candidate, schema))
        {
            return false;
        }
        let schema_changed = !self
            .selected_database_schema
            .is_some_and(|current| std::ptr::eq(current, schema));
        self.selected_database_schema = Some(schema);
        if schema_changed {
            self.visual_generation = self.visual_generation.wrapping_add(1).max(1);
        }
        self.selected_visual_node = None;
        self.selected_schema_parameter = None;
        self.refresh_visual_rows();
        true
    }

    fn replace_visual_document(&mut self, document: VisualDocument) {
        let retained_schema = match document.kind() {
            VisualDocumentKind::Database(artifact) => self
                .selected_database_schema
                .filter(|schema| database_schema_matches_artifact(schema, artifact)),
            VisualDocumentKind::Nginx | VisualDocumentKind::Apache => None,
        };
        self.visual_document = document;
        self.selected_database_schema = retained_schema;
        self.selected_visual_node = None;
        self.selected_schema_parameter = None;
        self.visual_dirty = false;
        self.visual_generation = self.visual_generation.wrapping_add(1).max(1);
        self.refresh_visual_rows();
    }

    fn contains_sensitive_visual_values(&self) -> bool {
        self.visual_document.contains_sensitive_values()
            || self.visual_rows.iter().any(|row| row.sensitive)
    }

    fn can_copy_visual(&self) -> bool {
        self.visual_document.can_copy() && !self.visual_rows.iter().any(|row| row.sensitive)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
struct DatabaseAssignmentLease {
    activation_generation: u64,
    input_generation: u64,
    visual_generation: u64,
    visual_dirty: bool,
    selected_schema: Option<*const DatabaseConfigSchema>,
}

enum PendingDatabaseAssignmentState {
    NotAssignment,
    Consumed,
    Ready {
        assignment: DatabaseParameterAssignment,
        value_error: Option<DatabaseParameterAssignmentError>,
    },
    Ambiguous(DatabaseParameterAssignmentAmbiguity),
    Rejected(DatabaseParameterAssignmentError),
}

struct PendingDatabaseAssignment {
    lease: DatabaseAssignmentLease,
    state: PendingDatabaseAssignmentState,
}

fn preflight_database_parameter_assignment_document(
    model: &ConfigGeneratorModel,
    assignment: &DatabaseParameterAssignment,
) -> Result<VisualDocument, &'static str> {
    let target = assignment.target();
    let schema = target.schema();
    let artifact = artifacts_for(schema.binding.product)
        .find(|artifact| {
            artifact.product == schema.binding.product
                && artifact.key == schema.binding.artifact_key
                && artifact.component == schema.binding.component
        })
        .ok_or("目标配方绑定的数据库工件不存在")?;
    let target_kind = VisualDocumentKind::Database(artifact);
    if model.visual_dirty {
        if model.visual_document.kind() != target_kind {
            return Err("当前有另一工件的未另存本地草稿；请先另存草稿");
        }
        if !model
            .current_database_schema()
            .is_some_and(|current| std::ptr::eq(current, schema))
        {
            return Err(
                "当前未另存草稿绑定的 Schema/版本与赋值目标不一致；请先另存草稿或选择完全相同的 Schema",
            );
        }
    }

    let same_schema = model
        .current_database_schema()
        .is_some_and(|current| std::ptr::eq(current, schema));
    let base = if model.visual_document.kind() == target_kind && same_schema {
        model.visual_document.clone()
    } else {
        VisualDocument::parse_database(artifact, "")
            .map_err(|_| "无法创建仅包含目标参数的空白本地草稿")?
    };
    stage_database_parameter_goal_document(
        &base,
        schema,
        target.parameter_index(),
        assignment.draft(),
    )
}

fn unique_goal_subject_for_query(raw_query: &str) -> Option<ConfigurationGoalSubject> {
    let results = search_configuration_goals(raw_query).ok()?;
    let mut subjects = results.matches().iter().map(|matched| matched.subject());
    let first = subjects.next()?;
    subjects.all(|subject| subject == first).then_some(first)
}

fn catalog_product_matches(query: &str, label: &str) -> bool {
    let query = query.trim();
    query.is_empty() || label.to_lowercase().contains(&query.to_lowercase())
}

fn configuration_metadata_matches<'a>(
    query: &str,
    candidates: impl IntoIterator<Item = &'a str>,
) -> bool {
    let query = query.trim().to_lowercase();
    if query.is_empty() {
        return true;
    }
    let terms = query.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    candidates.into_iter().any(|candidate| {
        let candidate = candidate.to_lowercase();
        candidate.contains(&query) || terms.iter().any(|term| candidate.contains(term))
    })
}

fn nginx_configuration_matches(selection: NginxConfigurationSelection, query: &str) -> bool {
    match selection {
        NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            [
                "baseline",
                "基础配置",
                "静态站点",
                "反向代理",
                "域名",
                "监听端口",
                "上游地址",
            ],
        ),
        NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            feature.aliases().iter().copied().chain([
                feature.key(),
                feature.title(),
                feature.summary(),
                feature.scope(),
            ]),
        ),
    }
}

fn apache_configuration_matches(selection: ApacheConfigurationSelection, query: &str) -> bool {
    match selection {
        ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            [
                "baseline",
                "基础配置",
                "静态站点",
                "反向代理",
                "virtualhost",
                "域名",
                "监听端口",
                "documentroot",
                "proxypass",
            ],
        ),
        ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            feature.aliases().iter().copied().chain([
                feature.key(),
                feature.title(),
                feature.summary(),
                feature.scope(),
            ]),
        ),
    }
}

fn tomcat_configuration_matches(selection: TomcatConfigurationSelection, query: &str) -> bool {
    match selection {
        TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            TomcatBaselineInvariant::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .flat_map(|invariant| {
                    invariant.aliases().iter().copied().chain([
                        invariant.key(),
                        invariant.title(),
                        invariant.summary(),
                        invariant.scope(),
                    ])
                })
                .chain([
                    "baseline",
                    "基础配置",
                    "Tomcat 9",
                    "Tomcat 10.1",
                    "Tomcat 11",
                    "Servlet",
                    "javax",
                    "jakarta",
                    "application name",
                    "connectionTimeout",
                ]),
        ),
        TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => configuration_metadata_matches(
            query,
            feature.aliases().iter().copied().chain([
                feature.key(),
                feature.title(),
                feature.summary(),
                feature.scope(),
            ]),
        ),
    }
}

                .default_value(DEFAULT_TOMCAT_ACCEPT_COUNT)
                .placeholder("1 - 100000")
        });
        let tomcat_max_connections_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .default_value(DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_CONNECTIONS)
                .placeholder("1 - 1000000")
        });
        let tomcat_max_post_size_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .default_value(DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_POST_SIZE)
                .placeholder("1 - 1073741824 字节")
        });
        let tomcat_max_swallow_size_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .default_value(DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_SWALLOW_SIZE)
                .placeholder("1 - 1073741824 字节")
        });
        let tomcat_compression_min_size_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .default_value(DEFAULT_TOMCAT_COMPRESSION_MIN_SIZE)
                .placeholder("1 - 1048576 字节")
        });
        let visual_search_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .placeholder("搜索安全名称与静态说明（最多 128 字符）")
                .validate(|value, _| value.chars().count() <= MAX_VISUAL_SEARCH_CHARS)
        });
        let database_assignment_value_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .placeholder("确认前可单独修改这个公开配置值")
                .validate(|value, _| value.len() <= MAX_DATABASE_CONFIG_SCHEMA_INPUT_BYTES)
        });
        let database_goal_value_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .placeholder("输入这个配置目标的新值")
                .validate(|value, _| value.len() <= MAX_DATABASE_CONFIG_SCHEMA_INPUT_BYTES)
        });
        let visual_name_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx).placeholder("例如 listen、location、第三方指令名")
        });
        let visual_arguments_input = cx.new(|cx| {
            InputState::new(window, cx)
                .multi_line(true)
                .placeholder("指令/块参数；选择注释时填写注释正文")
        });
        let initial_features = default_nginx_features(NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
        let generated = generate_from_values_with_features(
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            DEFAULT_SERVER_NAME,
            DEFAULT_LISTEN_PORT,
            DEFAULT_STATIC_ROOT,
            DEFAULT_UPSTREAM,
            NginxFeatureInputs {
                features: &initial_features,
                forbidden_path: None,
                allowed_source: DEFAULT_ALLOWED_SOURCE,
            },
        )
        .expect("checked-in Nginx generator defaults must remain valid");

        let mut subscriptions = vec![cx.observe(&shared, |_, _, cx| cx.notify())];
        for input in [
            &server_name_input,
            &listen_port_input,
            &static_root_input,
            &upstream_input,
            &forbidden_path_input,
            &allowed_source_input,
            &mysql_remote_listener_input,
            &mysql_max_connections_input,
            &mysql_connect_timeout_input,
            &mysql_max_connect_errors_input,
            &mysql_wait_timeout_input,
            &mysql_interactive_timeout_input,
            &mysql_long_query_time_input,
            &mysql_log_error_verbosity_input,
            &mysql_server_id_input,
            &mysql_binlog_retention_input,
            &postgresql_port_input,
            &postgresql_max_connections_input,
            &postgresql_superuser_reserved_connections_input,
            &postgresql_reserved_connections_input,
            &postgresql_authentication_timeout_input,
            &postgresql_idle_in_transaction_timeout_input,
            &postgresql_idle_session_timeout_input,
            &postgresql_temporary_file_limit_input,
            &postgresql_lock_wait_threshold_input,
            &postgresql_autovacuum_log_threshold_input,
            &postgresql_temporary_file_log_threshold_input,
            &mariadb_port_input,
            &mariadb_max_connections_input,
            &mariadb_connect_timeout_input,
            &mariadb_wait_timeout_input,
            &mariadb_interactive_timeout_input,
            &mariadb_max_connect_errors_input,
            &mariadb_max_allowed_packet_input,
            &mariadb_slow_query_time_input,
            &mariadb_server_id_input,
            &mariadb_binlog_retention_input,
            &mariadb_innodb_lock_wait_timeout_input,
            &redis_data_directory_input,
            &redis_port_input,
            &redis_max_clients_input,
            &redis_idle_timeout_input,
            &redis_tcp_keepalive_input,
            &redis_maxmemory_input,
            &redis_slowlog_threshold_input,
            &redis_slowlog_length_input,
            &redis_latency_threshold_input,
            &tomcat_application_name_input,
            &tomcat_port_input,
            &tomcat_connection_timeout_input,
            &tomcat_max_threads_input,
            &tomcat_accept_count_input,
            &tomcat_max_connections_input,
            &tomcat_max_post_size_input,
            &tomcat_max_swallow_size_input,
            &tomcat_compression_min_size_input,
        ] {
            subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
                input,
                window,
                |page, _, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
                    if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                        page.regenerate(cx);
                    }
                },
            ));
        }
        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
            &nginx_goal_search_input,
            window,
            |page, _, event: &InputEvent, window, cx| {
                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                    page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                }
            },
        ));
        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
            &catalog_product_search_input,
            window,
            |_, _, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                    cx.notify();
                }
            },
        ));
        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
            &visual_search_input,
            window,
            |page, _, event: &InputEvent, window, cx| {
                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                    page.handle_visual_search_change(window, cx);
                }
            },
        ));
        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
            &database_assignment_value_input,
            window,
            |page, _, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                    page.handle_database_assignment_value_change(cx);
                }
            },
        ));
        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(
            &database_goal_value_input,
            window,
            |page, _, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
                    page.error = None;
                    page.notice = None;
                    cx.notify();
                }
            },
        ));

        Self {
            services,
            tokio,
            shared,
            model: ConfigGeneratorModel::new(generated),
            server_name_input,
            listen_port_input,
            static_root_input,
            upstream_input,
            nginx_goal_search_input,
            catalog_product_search_input,
            forbidden_path_input,
            allowed_source_input,
            mysql_remote_listener_input,
            mysql_max_connections_input,
            mysql_connect_timeout_input,
            mysql_max_connect_errors_input,
            mysql_wait_timeout_input,
            mysql_interactive_timeout_input,
            mysql_long_query_time_input,
            mysql_log_error_verbosity_input,
            mysql_server_id_input,
            mysql_binlog_retention_input,
            postgresql_port_input,
            postgresql_max_connections_input,
            postgresql_superuser_reserved_connections_input,
            postgresql_reserved_connections_input,
            postgresql_authentication_timeout_input,
            postgresql_idle_in_transaction_timeout_input,
            postgresql_idle_session_timeout_input,
            postgresql_temporary_file_limit_input,
            postgresql_lock_wait_threshold_input,
            postgresql_autovacuum_log_threshold_input,
            postgresql_temporary_file_log_threshold_input,
            mariadb_port_input,
            mariadb_max_connections_input,
            mariadb_connect_timeout_input,
            mariadb_wait_timeout_input,
            mariadb_interactive_timeout_input,
            mariadb_max_connect_errors_input,
            mariadb_max_allowed_packet_input,
            mariadb_slow_query_time_input,
            mariadb_server_id_input,
            mariadb_binlog_retention_input,
            mariadb_innodb_lock_wait_timeout_input,
            redis_data_directory_input,
            redis_port_input,
            redis_max_clients_input,
            redis_idle_timeout_input,
            redis_tcp_keepalive_input,
            redis_maxmemory_input,
            redis_slowlog_threshold_input,
            redis_slowlog_length_input,
            redis_latency_threshold_input,
            tomcat_application_name_input,
            tomcat_port_input,
            tomcat_connection_timeout_input,
            tomcat_max_threads_input,
            tomcat_accept_count_input,
            tomcat_max_connections_input,
            tomcat_max_post_size_input,
            tomcat_max_swallow_size_input,
            tomcat_compression_min_size_input,
            visual_search_input,
            database_assignment_value_input,
            database_goal_value_input,
            visual_name_input,
            visual_arguments_input,
            page_scroll: ScrollHandle::new(),
            quick_preview_scroll: ScrollHandle::new(),
            visual_node_scroll: UniformListScrollHandle::new(),
            visual_catalog_scroll: UniformListScrollHandle::new(),
            database_assignment_generation: 1,
            pending_database_assignment: PendingDatabaseAssignment {
                lease: DatabaseAssignmentLease {
                    activation_generation: 0,
                    input_generation: 1,
                    visual_generation: 1,
                    visual_dirty: false,
                    selected_schema: None,
                },
                state: PendingDatabaseAssignmentState::NotAssignment,
            },
            visual_editor_reset_pending: false,
            importing: false,
            import_abort: None,
            saving: false,
            save_abort: None,
            error: None,
            notice: None,
            _subscriptions: subscriptions,
        }
    }

    pub fn activate(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        self.model.activate();
        self.regenerate(cx);
        self.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
        if self.model.mode == ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick {
            self.server_name_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
        cx.notify();
    }

    pub fn deactivate(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if let Some(abort) = self.import_abort.take() {
            abort.abort();
        }
        if let Some(abort) = self.save_abort.take() {
            abort.abort();
        }
        self.importing = false;
        self.saving = false;
        self.model.deactivate();
        self.advance_database_assignment_generation();
        self.pending_database_assignment = PendingDatabaseAssignment {
            lease: self.database_assignment_lease(),
            state: PendingDatabaseAssignmentState::NotAssignment,
        };
        self.notice = None;
        cx.notify();
    }

    fn select_kind(&mut self, kind: NginxSiteKind, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.model.kind == kind {
            return;
        }
        self.model.kind = kind;
        self.model.active_nginx_configuration = NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.model
            .nginx_features
            .retain(|feature| feature.supports_kind(kind));
        self.model
            .nginx_features
            .extend(default_nginx_features(kind));
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match kind {
            NginxSiteKind::Static => &self.static_root_input,
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy => &self.upstream_input,
        };
        input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        cx.notify();
    }

    fn select_apache_kind(
        &mut self,
        kind: ApacheSiteKind,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if self.model.apache_kind == kind {
            return;
        }
        self.model.apache_kind = kind;
        self.model.active_apache_configuration = ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.model
            .apache_features
            .retain(|feature| feature.supports_kind(kind));
        self.model
            .apache_features
            .extend(default_apache_features(kind));
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match kind {
            ApacheSiteKind::Static => &self.static_root_input,
            ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy => &self.upstream_input,
        };
        input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        cx.notify();
    }

    fn select_goal_subject(
        &mut self,
        subject: ConfigurationGoalSubject,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.selected_goal_subject = subject;
        self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
        self.nginx_goal_search_input
            .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("", window, cx));
        self.quick_preview_scroll
            .set_offset(gpui::point(px(0.0), px(0.0)));
        self.regenerate(cx);
        cx.notify();
    }

    fn reset_goal_catalog_search(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.saving || self.importing {
            return;
        }
        self.nginx_goal_search_input
            .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("", window, cx));
        self.error = None;
        self.notice = None;
        let selected_subject = self.model.selected_goal_subject;
        let (
            reset_web_values,
            reset_mysql_values,
            reset_postgresql_values,
            reset_mariadb_values,
            reset_redis_values,
            reset_tomcat_values,
        ) = match selected_subject {
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.kind = NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy;
                self.model.nginx_features = default_nginx_features(NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
                self.model.active_nginx_configuration = NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model.forbidden_path_scope = NginxForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants;
                (true, false, false, false, false, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.apache_kind = ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy;
                self.model.apache_features = default_apache_features(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
                self.model.active_apache_configuration = ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope =
                    ApacheForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants;
                (true, false, false, false, false, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.mysql_features = default_mysql_features();
                self.model.active_mysql_configuration = MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                (false, true, false, false, false, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.postgresql_version = PostgreSqlVersion::V18;
                self.model.postgresql_features = default_postgresql_features();
                self.model.active_postgresql_configuration =
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model.postgresql_wal_compression = PostgreSqlWalCompression::Pglz;
                (false, false, true, false, false, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.mariadb_version = MariaDbVersion::V12_3;
                self.model.mariadb_features = default_mariadb_features();
                self.model.active_mariadb_configuration = MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model.mariadb_character_profile =
                    MariaDbCharacterProfile::UnicodeCaseInsensitive;
                (false, false, false, true, false, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.redis_version = RedisVersion::V8_10;
                self.model.redis_bind_profile = RedisBindProfile::DualStackLoopback;
                self.model.redis_features = default_redis_features();
                self.model.active_redis_configuration = RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model.redis_eviction_policy = RedisEvictionPolicy::NoEviction;
                self.model.redis_append_fsync = RedisAppendFsync::EverySecond;
                (false, false, false, false, true, false)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat) => {
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.tomcat_version = TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0;
                self.model.tomcat_connector_address = TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv4Loopback;
                self.model.tomcat_features = default_tomcat_features();
                self.model.active_tomcat_configuration = TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                (false, false, false, false, false, true)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(_) => (false, false, false, false, false, false),
        };
        if reset_web_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (&self.server_name_input, DEFAULT_SERVER_NAME),
                (&self.listen_port_input, DEFAULT_LISTEN_PORT),
                (&self.static_root_input, DEFAULT_STATIC_ROOT),
                (&self.upstream_input, DEFAULT_UPSTREAM),
                (&self.forbidden_path_input, DEFAULT_FORBIDDEN_PATH),
                (&self.allowed_source_input, DEFAULT_ALLOWED_SOURCE),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_mysql_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (
                    &self.mysql_remote_listener_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_REMOTE_LISTENER,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mysql_max_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mysql_connect_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mysql_max_connect_errors_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_MAX_CONNECT_ERRORS,
                ),
                (&self.mysql_wait_timeout_input, DEFAULT_MYSQL_WAIT_TIMEOUT),
                (
                    &self.mysql_interactive_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_INTERACTIVE_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mysql_long_query_time_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_LONG_QUERY_TIME,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mysql_log_error_verbosity_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_LOG_ERROR_VERBOSITY,
                ),
                (&self.mysql_server_id_input, DEFAULT_MYSQL_SERVER_ID),
                (
                    &self.mysql_binlog_retention_input,
                    DEFAULT_MYSQL_BINLOG_RETENTION,
                ),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_postgresql_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (&self.postgresql_port_input, DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_PORT),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_max_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_superuser_reserved_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_SUPERUSER_RESERVED_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_reserved_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_RESERVED_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_authentication_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_AUTHENTICATION_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_idle_in_transaction_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_IDLE_IN_TRANSACTION_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_idle_session_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_IDLE_SESSION_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_temporary_file_limit_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_TEMPORARY_FILE_LIMIT_MIB,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_lock_wait_threshold_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_LOCK_WAIT_THRESHOLD_MILLISECONDS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_autovacuum_log_threshold_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_AUTOVACUUM_LOG_THRESHOLD_MILLISECONDS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.postgresql_temporary_file_log_threshold_input,
                    DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_TEMPORARY_FILE_LOG_THRESHOLD_KILOBYTES,
                ),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_mariadb_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (&self.mariadb_port_input, DEFAULT_MARIADB_PORT),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_max_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_connect_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_wait_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_interactive_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_INTERACTIVE_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_max_connect_errors_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_CONNECT_ERRORS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_max_allowed_packet_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_ALLOWED_PACKET_MIB,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_slow_query_time_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_SLOW_QUERY_TIME,
                ),
                (&self.mariadb_server_id_input, DEFAULT_MARIADB_SERVER_ID),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_binlog_retention_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_BINLOG_RETENTION,
                ),
                (
                    &self.mariadb_innodb_lock_wait_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_INNODB_LOCK_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
                ),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_redis_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (
                    &self.redis_data_directory_input,
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_DATA_DIRECTORY,
                ),
                (&self.redis_port_input, DEFAULT_REDIS_PORT),
                (&self.redis_max_clients_input, DEFAULT_REDIS_MAX_CLIENTS),
                (&self.redis_idle_timeout_input, DEFAULT_REDIS_IDLE_TIMEOUT),
                (&self.redis_tcp_keepalive_input, DEFAULT_REDIS_TCP_KEEPALIVE),
                (&self.redis_maxmemory_input, DEFAULT_REDIS_MAXMEMORY_MIB),
                (
                    &self.redis_slowlog_threshold_input,
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_SLOWLOG_THRESHOLD,
                ),
                (
                    &self.redis_slowlog_length_input,
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_SLOWLOG_LENGTH,
                ),
                (
                    &self.redis_latency_threshold_input,
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_LATENCY_THRESHOLD,
                ),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_tomcat_values {
            for (input, value) in [
                (
                    &self.tomcat_application_name_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_APPLICATION_NAME,
                ),
                (&self.tomcat_port_input, DEFAULT_TOMCAT_PORT),
                (
                    &self.tomcat_connection_timeout_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT,
                ),
                (&self.tomcat_max_threads_input, DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_THREADS),
                (&self.tomcat_accept_count_input, DEFAULT_TOMCAT_ACCEPT_COUNT),
                (
                    &self.tomcat_max_connections_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                ),
                (
                    &self.tomcat_max_post_size_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_POST_SIZE,
                ),
                (
                    &self.tomcat_max_swallow_size_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_SWALLOW_SIZE,
                ),
                (
                    &self.tomcat_compression_min_size_input,
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_COMPRESSION_MIN_SIZE,
                ),
            ] {
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
            }
        }
        if reset_web_values
            || reset_mysql_values
            || reset_postgresql_values
            || reset_mariadb_values
            || reset_redis_values
            || reset_tomcat_values
        {
            self.quick_preview_scroll
                .set_offset(gpui::point(px(0.0), px(0.0)));
            self.regenerate(cx);
        }
        self.nginx_goal_search_input
            .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        cx.notify();
    }

    fn start_curated_configuration_goal(
        &mut self,
        action: ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction,
        query: &str,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        match action {
            ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxStaticSite
            | ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxReverseProxy => {
                let kind = match action {
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxStaticSite => NginxSiteKind::Static,
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxReverseProxy => NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxForbiddenPath403
                    | ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheForbiddenPath403
                    | ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheDisableTrace
                    | ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheReduceVersionDisclosure => {
                        unreachable!()
                    }
                };
                self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
                self.model.kind = kind;
                self.model.active_nginx_configuration = NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
                self.model
                    .nginx_features
                    .retain(|feature| feature.supports_kind(kind));
                self.model
                    .nginx_features
                    .extend(default_nginx_features(kind));
                self.regenerate(cx);
                let input = match kind {
                    NginxSiteKind::Static => &self.static_root_input,
                    NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy => &self.upstream_input,
                };
                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
                self.notice = Some(
                    format!(
                        "已切换 Baseline：{}。已选择的兼容配置会继续叠加；保存前请检查完整预览，部署前仍需 nginx -t 与真实请求验证。",
                        kind.label()
                    )
                    .into(),
                );
                self.error = None;
                cx.notify();
            }
            ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxForbiddenPath403 => {
                self.start_forbidden_path_goal(query, window, cx)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheForbiddenPath403 => {
                self.start_apache_forbidden_path_goal(query, window, cx)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheDisableTrace => {
                self.start_apache_feature_goal(ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace, window, cx)
            }
            ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheReduceVersionDisclosure => self
                .start_apache_feature_goal(ApacheSiteFeature::ReduceVersionDisclosure, window, cx),
        }
    }

    fn start_apache_feature_goal(
        &mut self,
        feature: ApacheSiteFeature,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.selected_goal_subject =
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd);
        self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
        self.open_apache_configuration(ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature), window, cx);
    }

    fn start_apache_forbidden_path_goal(
        &mut self,
        query: &str,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let Some(path) = nginx_forbidden_path_from_goal_query(query) else {
            self.model.generated = None;
            self.error = Some(
                "请在目标描述中写入一个规范绝对 URI 路径，例如：Apache /admin 返回 403。".into(),
            );
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        let path = path.as_str().to_owned();
        self.forbidden_path_input
            .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(path.clone(), window, cx));
        self.model.selected_goal_subject =
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd);
        self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
        self.model
            .apache_features
            .insert(ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
        self.model.active_apache_configuration =
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
        self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope = ApacheForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants;
        self.regenerate(cx);
        if self.model.generated.is_some() {
            self.forbidden_path_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn open_nginx_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: NginxConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_nginx_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        if !nginx_feature_applicable(
            feature,
            self.model.kind,
            self.upstream_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
        ) {
            self.error = Some(if feature == NginxSiteFeature::HttpsUpstreamSni {
                "请先把上游地址改为使用 DNS 主机名的 https:// origin，再启用“仅发送 SNI”。该配置不会校验证书或验证上游身份。"
                    .into()
            } else {
                format!(
                    "{}不适用于当前{}的 Baseline。",
                    feature.title(),
                    self.model.kind.label()
                )
                .into()
            });
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        }
        self.model.active_nginx_configuration = selection;
        match feature {
            NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback => {
                self.model
                    .nginx_features
                    .remove(&NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles);
            }
            NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles => {
                self.model
                    .nginx_features
                    .remove(&NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback);
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        self.model.nginx_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        match feature {
            NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403 => self
                .forbidden_path_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx)),
            NginxSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp => self
                .allowed_source_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx)),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn remove_nginx_configuration(&mut self, feature: NginxSiteFeature, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        self.model.nginx_features.remove(&feature);
        if feature == NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback && self.model.kind == NginxSiteKind::Static {
            self.model
                .nginx_features
                .insert(NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles);
        }
        self.model.active_nginx_configuration = NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_apache_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: ApacheConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_apache_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        if !feature.supports_kind(self.model.apache_kind) {
            self.error = Some(
                format!(
                    "{}不适用于当前{}的 Baseline。",
                    feature.title(),
                    self.model.apache_kind.label()
                )
                .into(),
            );
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        }
        self.model.active_apache_configuration = selection;
        if feature == ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods {
            self.model
                .apache_features
                .insert(ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace);
        }
        self.model.apache_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        match feature {
            ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403 => self
                .forbidden_path_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx)),
            ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp => self
                .allowed_source_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx)),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn remove_apache_configuration(&mut self, feature: ApacheSiteFeature, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        self.model.apache_features.remove(&feature);
        if feature == ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace {
            self.model
                .apache_features
                .remove(&ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods);
        }
        self.model.active_apache_configuration = ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_mysql_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: MySqlConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_mysql_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        self.model.active_mysql_configuration = selection;
        match feature {
            MySqlConfigFeature::RemoteListener => {
                self.model
                    .mysql_features
                    .remove(&MySqlConfigFeature::DisableTcp);
            }
            MySqlConfigFeature::DisableTcp => {
                self.model
                    .mysql_features
                    .remove(&MySqlConfigFeature::RemoteListener);
            }
            MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention => {
                self.model
                    .mysql_features
                    .insert(MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging);
            }
            MySqlConfigFeature::SkipNameResolve
            | MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections
            | MySqlConfigFeature::ConnectTimeout
            | MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnectErrors
            | MySqlConfigFeature::SessionTimeouts
            | MySqlConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog
            | MySqlConfigFeature::GeneralLog
            | MySqlConfigFeature::LogErrorVerbosity
            | MySqlConfigFeature::DisableFileImportExport
            | MySqlConfigFeature::ReadOnlyReplica
            | MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging
            | MySqlConfigFeature::EnableLocalInfile => {}
        }
        self.model.mysql_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match feature {
            MySqlConfigFeature::RemoteListener => Some(&self.mysql_remote_listener_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections => Some(&self.mysql_max_connections_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::ConnectTimeout => Some(&self.mysql_connect_timeout_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnectErrors => Some(&self.mysql_max_connect_errors_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::SessionTimeouts => Some(&self.mysql_wait_timeout_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog => Some(&self.mysql_long_query_time_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::LogErrorVerbosity => Some(&self.mysql_log_error_verbosity_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging => Some(&self.mysql_server_id_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention => Some(&self.mysql_binlog_retention_input),
            MySqlConfigFeature::DisableTcp
            | MySqlConfigFeature::SkipNameResolve
            | MySqlConfigFeature::GeneralLog
            | MySqlConfigFeature::DisableFileImportExport
            | MySqlConfigFeature::ReadOnlyReplica
            | MySqlConfigFeature::EnableLocalInfile => None,
        };
        if let Some(input) = input {
            input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn remove_mysql_configuration(&mut self, feature: MySqlConfigFeature, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        self.model.mysql_features.remove(&feature);
        if feature == MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging {
            self.model
                .mysql_features
                .remove(&MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention);
        }
        self.model.active_mysql_configuration = MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_postgresql_version(
        &mut self,
        version: PostgreSqlVersion,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if self.model.postgresql_version == version {
            return;
        }
        self.model.postgresql_version = version;
        if !version.supports_reserved_connections() {
            self.postgresql_reserved_connections_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value(DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_RESERVED_CONNECTIONS, window, cx)
                });
        }
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_postgresql_wal_compression(
        &mut self,
        compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.postgresql_wal_compression = compression;
        self.model
            .postgresql_features
            .insert(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::WalCompression);
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_postgresql_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_postgresql_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        if !feature.supports_version(self.model.postgresql_version) {
            self.error = Some(
                format!(
                    "{} 不支持 PostgreSQL {}；配置未添加。",
                    feature.title(),
                    self.model.postgresql_version.label()
                )
                .into(),
            );
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        }
        self.model.active_postgresql_configuration = selection;
        self.model.postgresql_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match feature {
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_max_connections_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AuthenticationTimeout => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_authentication_timeout_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleInTransactionGuard => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_idle_in_transaction_timeout_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleSessionGuard => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_idle_session_timeout_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLimit => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_temporary_file_limit_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::LockWaitLogging => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_lock_wait_threshold_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AutovacuumLogging => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_autovacuum_log_threshold_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLogging => {
                Some(&self.postgresql_temporary_file_log_threshold_input)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionLifecycleLogging
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::CheckpointLogging
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IoTiming
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::WalCompression
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::QueryIdentifier => None,
        };
        if let Some(input) = input {
            input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn remove_postgresql_configuration(
        &mut self,
        feature: PostgreSqlConfigFeature,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.postgresql_features.remove(&feature);
        self.model.active_postgresql_configuration = PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_mariadb_version(&mut self, version: MariaDbVersion, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.model.mariadb_version == version {
            return;
        }
        self.model.mariadb_version = version;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_mariadb_character_profile(
        &mut self,
        profile: MariaDbCharacterProfile,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.mariadb_character_profile = profile;
        self.model
            .mariadb_features
            .insert(MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation);
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_mariadb_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: MariaDbConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_mariadb_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        if !feature.supports_version(self.model.mariadb_version) {
            self.error = Some(
                format!(
                    "{} 不支持 MariaDB {}；配置未添加。",
                    feature.title(),
                    self.model.mariadb_version.label()
                )
                .into(),
            );
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        }
        self.model.active_mariadb_configuration = selection;
        if let Some(required) = feature.required_feature() {
            self.model.mariadb_features.insert(required);
        }
        self.model.mariadb_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match feature {
            MariaDbConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity => Some(&self.mariadb_max_connections_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::HandshakeTimeout => Some(&self.mariadb_connect_timeout_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SessionIdleTimeouts => Some(&self.mariadb_wait_timeout_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::HostErrorThreshold => {
                Some(&self.mariadb_max_connect_errors_input)
            }
            MariaDbConfigFeature::MaximumPacketSize => Some(&self.mariadb_max_allowed_packet_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog => Some(&self.mariadb_slow_query_time_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging => Some(&self.mariadb_server_id_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention => Some(&self.mariadb_binlog_retention_input),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbLockWaitTimeout => {
                Some(&self.mariadb_innodb_lock_wait_timeout_input)
            }
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SkipNameResolve
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::ErrorLogFile
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogDurability
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbDurability
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbDeadlockLogging => None,
        };
        if let Some(input) = input {
            input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn remove_mariadb_configuration(
        &mut self,
        feature: MariaDbConfigFeature,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.mariadb_features.remove(&feature);
        if feature == MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging {
            self.model
                .mariadb_features
                .remove(&MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention);
            self.model
                .mariadb_features
                .remove(&MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogDurability);
        }
        self.model.active_mariadb_configuration = MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn redis_feature_removable(&self, feature: RedisConfigFeature) -> bool {
        match feature {
            RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit => false,
            RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence => self
                .model
                .redis_features
                .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence),
            RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence => self
                .model
                .redis_features
                .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence),
            RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients
            | RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout
            | RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive
            | RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy
            | RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog
            | RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor => true,
        }
    }

    fn select_redis_version(&mut self, version: RedisVersion, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.model.redis_version == version {
            return;
        }
        self.model.redis_version = version;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_redis_bind_profile(&mut self, profile: RedisBindProfile, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.model.redis_bind_profile == profile {
            return;
        }
        self.model.redis_bind_profile = profile;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_redis_eviction_policy(
        &mut self,
        policy: RedisEvictionPolicy,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.redis_eviction_policy = policy;
        self.model
            .redis_features
            .insert(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit);
        self.model
            .redis_features
            .insert(RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy);
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_redis_append_fsync(&mut self, policy: RedisAppendFsync, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        self.model.redis_append_fsync = policy;
        self.model
            .redis_features
            .insert(RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence);
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_redis_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: RedisConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_redis_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        self.model.active_redis_configuration = selection;
        if feature == RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy {
            self.model
                .redis_features
                .insert(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit);
        }
        self.model.redis_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match feature {
            RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients => Some(&self.redis_max_clients_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout => Some(&self.redis_idle_timeout_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive => Some(&self.redis_tcp_keepalive_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit => Some(&self.redis_maxmemory_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog => Some(&self.redis_slowlog_threshold_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor => Some(&self.redis_latency_threshold_input),
            RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy
            | RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence
            | RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence => None,
        };
        if let Some(input) = input {
            input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn remove_redis_configuration(&mut self, feature: RedisConfigFeature, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if !self.redis_feature_removable(feature) {
            self.error = Some(
                match feature {
                    RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit => {
                        "新建空实例 Baseline 必须保留非零 maxmemory；请修改容量，不能移除。"
                    }
                    RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence | RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence => {
                        "AOF 与 RDB 至少保留一种；请先启用另一种持久化方式。"
                    }
                    RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients
                    | RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout
                    | RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive
                    | RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy
                    | RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog
                    | RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor => unreachable!(),
                }
                .into(),
            );
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        }
        self.model.redis_features.remove(&feature);
        self.model.active_redis_configuration = RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_tomcat_version(&mut self, version: TomcatVersionFamily, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        if self.model.tomcat_version == version {
            return;
        }
        self.model.tomcat_version = version;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_tomcat_connector_address(
        &mut self,
        address: TomcatConnectorAddress,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if self.model.tomcat_connector_address == address {
            return;
        }
        self.model.tomcat_connector_address = address;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn open_tomcat_configuration(
        &mut self,
        selection: TomcatConfigurationSelection,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        let TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) = selection else {
            self.model.active_tomcat_configuration = selection;
            self.error = None;
            self.notice = None;
            cx.notify();
            return;
        };
        self.model.active_tomcat_configuration = selection;
        match feature {
            TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly => {
                self.model
                    .tomcat_features
                    .remove(&TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests);
            }
            TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests => {
                self.model
                    .tomcat_features
                    .remove(&TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly);
            }
            TomcatConfigFeature::ConnectorLimits
            | TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HostUnpackWars
            | TomcatConfigFeature::AllowCrossContext
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HttpOnlySessionCookies
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HeaderSecurityFilter => {}
        }
        self.model.tomcat_features.insert(feature);
        self.regenerate(cx);
        let input = match feature {
            TomcatConfigFeature::ConnectorLimits => Some(&self.tomcat_max_threads_input),
            TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression => {
                Some(&self.tomcat_compression_min_size_input)
            }
            TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HostUnpackWars
            | TomcatConfigFeature::AllowCrossContext
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HttpOnlySessionCookies
            | TomcatConfigFeature::HeaderSecurityFilter
            | TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly
            | TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests => None,
        };
        if let Some(input) = input {
            input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
        }
    }

    fn remove_tomcat_configuration(
        &mut self,
        feature: TomcatConfigFeature,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        self.model.tomcat_features.remove(&feature);
        self.model.active_tomcat_configuration = TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn select_apache_forbidden_path_scope(
        &mut self,
        scope: ApacheForbiddenPathScope,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope == scope {
            return;
        }
        self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope = scope;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn start_forbidden_path_goal(
        &mut self,
        query: &str,
        window: &mut Window,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if let Some(path) = nginx_forbidden_goal_query_path(query) {
            self.forbidden_path_input
                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(path, window, cx));
        }
        self.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick;
        self.model
            .nginx_features
            .insert(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
        self.model.active_nginx_configuration =
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
        self.model.forbidden_path_scope = NginxForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants;
        self.regenerate(cx);
        self.forbidden_path_input
            .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
    }

    fn select_forbidden_path_scope(
        &mut self,
        scope: NginxForbiddenPathScope,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) {
        if self.model.forbidden_path_scope == scope {
            return;
        }
        self.model.forbidden_path_scope = scope;
        self.regenerate(cx);
    }

    fn regenerate(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
        let selected_subject = self.model.selected_goal_subject;
        let result = match selected_subject {
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx) => {
                let forbidden_path = self
                    .model
                    .nginx_features
                    .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403)
                    .then(|| self.forbidden_path_input.read(cx).value().to_string());
                Some(
                    generate_from_values_with_features(
                        self.model.kind,
                        self.server_name_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.listen_port_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.static_root_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.upstream_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        NginxFeatureInputs {
                            features: &self.model.nginx_features,
                            forbidden_path: forbidden_path
                                .as_deref()
                                .map(|path| (path, self.model.forbidden_path_scope)),
                            allowed_source: self.allowed_source_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        },
                    )
                    .map(GeneratedQuickConfig::Nginx),
                )
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd) => {
                let forbidden_path = self
                    .model
                    .apache_features
                    .contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403)
                    .then(|| self.forbidden_path_input.read(cx).value().to_string());
                Some(
                    generate_apache_from_values_with_features(
                        self.model.apache_kind,
                        self.server_name_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.listen_port_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.static_root_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        self.upstream_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        ApacheFeatureInputs {
                            features: &self.model.apache_features,
                            forbidden_path: forbidden_path
                                .as_deref()
                                .map(|path| (path, self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope)),
                            allowed_source: self.allowed_source_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        },
                    )
                    .map(GeneratedQuickConfig::Apache),
                )
            }
            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql) => Some(
                generate_mysql_from_values_with_features(
                    &self.model.mysql_features,
                    MySqlFeatureValueInputs {
                        remote_listener: self.mysql_remote_listener_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        max_connections: self.mysql_max_connections_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        connect_timeout: self.mysql_connect_timeout_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        max_connect_errors: self
                            .mysql_max_connect_errors_input
                            .read(cx)
                            .value()
                            .as_ref(),
                        wait_timeout: self.mysql_wait_timeout_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                        interactive_timeout: self
                            .mysql_interactive_timeout_input
                            .read(cx)
                            .value()
                            .as_ref(),
                        long_query_time: self.mysql_long_query_time_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
