                        .when_some(*value_error, |card, error| {
                            card.child(
                                div()
                                    .rounded_md()
                                    .bg(tokens.surface)
                                    .p_2()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                    .child(format!(
                                        "Blocked：{}",
                                        database_parameter_assignment_error_message(error)
                                    )),
                            )
                        })
                        .when_some(normalized_preview, |card, preview| {
                            card.child(
                                div()
                                    .rounded_md()
                                    .bg(tokens.surface)
                                    .p_2()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .child(format!("待确认写入：{preview}")),
                            )
                        })
                        .when_some(preflight_error, |card, message| {
                            card.child(
                                div()
                                    .id("config-generator-database-assignment-preflight-blocked")
                                    .debug_selector(|| {
                                        "config-generator-database-assignment-preflight-blocked"
                                            .to_owned()
                                    })
                                    .rounded_md()
                                    .bg(tokens.surface)
                                    .p_2()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                    .child(format!("Blocked：{message}。原文档保持不变。")),
                            )
                        })
                        .when(!lease_current, |card| {
                            card.child(
                                div()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                    .child(
                                        "页面、搜索值或文档状态已变化；请重新编辑目标描述以刷新预览。",
                                    ),
                            )
                        })
                        .child(
                            crate::buttons::button(
                                "config-generator-confirm-database-assignment",
                            )
                            .label("确认写入本地草稿")
                            .small()
                            .primary()
                            .disabled(busy || !actionable)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                page.confirm_database_parameter_assignment(lease, window, cx)
                            })),
                        )
                        .child(
                            div()
                                .text_xs()
                                .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                .child(
                                    "确认只修改本地草稿；不会连接数据库、写远端、reload/restart 或宣称目标已验证。",
                                ),
                        )
                        .into_any_element(),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fn render_goal_subject_button(
        &self,
        id: SharedString,
        subject: ConfigurationGoalSubject,
        _tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        crate::buttons::button(id)
            .label(subject.label())
            .small()
            .ghost()
            .w_full()
            .justify_start()
            .selected(self.model.selected_goal_subject == subject)
            .on_click(
                cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                    page.select_goal_subject(subject, window, cx)
                }),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_goal_product_catalog(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let query = self
            .catalog_product_search_input
            .read(cx)
            .value()
            .to_string();
        let web_products = WEB_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|product| catalog_product_matches(&query, product.label()))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let application_products = APPLICATION_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|product| catalog_product_matches(&query, product.label()))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let cache_products = CACHE_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|product| catalog_product_matches(&query, product.label()))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let database_products = DATABASE_CONFIG_PRODUCTS
            .iter()
            .filter(|definition| catalog_product_matches(&query, definition.label))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let visible_count = web_products.len()
            + application_products.len()
            + cache_products.len()
            + database_products.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-goal-product-catalog")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-goal-product-catalog".to_owned())
            .w(px(240.0))
            .h_full()
            .flex_none()
            .min_h_0()
            .rounded_lg()
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex_none()
                    .p_3()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child("1. 选择中间件（树结构）"))
                    .child(Input::new(&self.catalog_product_search_input).small()),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-goal-product-list")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-goal-product-list".to_owned())
                    .flex_1()
                    .min_h_0()
                    .overflow_y_scrollbar()
                    .p_2()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .when(!web_products.is_empty(), |list| {
                        list.child(
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .flex_col()
                                .gap_1()
                                .child(
                                    div()
                                        .px_2()
                                        .py_1()
                                        .text_xs()
                                        .font_semibold()
                                        .text_color(tokens.text)
                                        .child("⌄  反向代理 / Web 服务器"),
                                )
                                .child(div().ml_3().flex().flex_col().gap_1().children(
                                    web_products.into_iter().map(|product| {
                                        self.render_goal_subject_button(
                                            SharedString::from(format!(
                                                "config-generator-goal-product-{product:?}"
                                            )),
                                            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(product),
                                            tokens,
                                            cx,
                                        )
                                    }),
                                )),
                        )
                    })
                    .when(!application_products.is_empty(), |list| {
                        list.child(
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .flex_col()
                                .gap_1()
                                .child(
                                    div()
                                        .px_2()
                                        .py_1()
                                        .text_xs()
                                        .font_semibold()
                                        .child("⌄  应用服务器"),
                                )
                                .child(div().ml_3().flex().flex_col().gap_1().children(
                                    application_products.into_iter().map(|product| {
                                        self.render_goal_subject_button(
                                            SharedString::from(format!(
                                                "config-generator-goal-product-{product:?}"
                                            )),
                                            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(product),
                                            tokens,
                                            cx,
                                        )
                                    }),
                                )),
                        )
                    })
                    .when(!cache_products.is_empty(), |list| {
                        list.child(
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .flex_col()
                                .gap_1()
                                .child(
                                    div()
                                        .px_2()
                                        .py_1()
                                        .text_xs()
                                        .font_semibold()
                                        .child("⌄  缓存"),
                                )
                                .child(div().ml_3().flex().flex_col().gap_1().children(
                                    cache_products.into_iter().map(|product| {
                                        self.render_goal_subject_button(
                                            SharedString::from(format!(
                                                "config-generator-goal-product-{product:?}"
                                            )),
                                            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(product),
                                            tokens,
                                            cx,
                                        )
                                    }),
                                )),
                        )
                    })
                    .when(!database_products.is_empty(), |list| {
                        list.child(
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .flex_col()
                                .gap_1()
                                .child(
                                    div()
                                        .px_2()
                                        .py_1()
                                        .text_xs()
                                        .font_semibold()
                                        .child("⌄  数据库"),
                                )
                                .child(div().ml_3().flex().flex_col().gap_1().children(
                                    database_products.into_iter().map(|definition| {
                                        self.render_goal_subject_button(
                                            SharedString::from(format!(
                                                "config-generator-goal-product-{}",
                                                definition.key
                                            )),
                                            ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(definition.product),
                                            tokens,
                                            cx,
                                        )
                                    }),
                                )),
                        )
                    })
                    .when(visible_count == 0, |list| {
                        list.child(
                            div()
                                .p_3()
                                .text_xs()
                                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                .child("没有匹配的已登记产品。"),
                        )
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex_none()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_t_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("已登记产品 · {visible_count}")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_nginx_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            NginxSiteFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| nginx_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.nginx_features.len();
        let kind = self.model.kind;
        let upstream = self.upstream_input.read(cx).value().to_string();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .font_semibold()
                            .child(format!("Baseline 已加载：Nginx {}", kind.label())),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-configuration-table-header")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-configuration-table-header".to_owned())
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(92.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(46.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_nginx_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled, applicable) = match selection {
                            NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!("{} Baseline", kind.label()),
                                "提供 server、监听、域名及站点处理主流程；始终保留。".to_owned(),
                                "站点".to_owned(),
                                true,
                                true,
                            ),
                            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.scope().to_owned(),
                                self.model.nginx_features.contains(&feature),
                                nginx_feature_applicable(feature, kind, &upstream),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector =
                            format!("config-generator-configuration-row-{}", selection.key());
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_selector =
                            format!("config-generator-select-{}", selection.key());
                        let action_debug_selector = action_selector.clone();
                        let action_label = if !applicable {
                            "不适用"
                        } else if selection == NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline {
                            "编辑"
                        } else if enabled {
                            "已添加"
                        } else {
                            "选择"
                        };

                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(92.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(46.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    div()
                                        .debug_selector(move || action_debug_selector.clone())
                                        .child(
                                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(
                                                action_selector,
                                            ))
                                            .label(action_label)
                                            .small()
                                            .selected(enabled && active)
                                            .disabled(!applicable)
                                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                                page.open_nginx_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                            })),
                                        ),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_nginx_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终包含")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_nginx_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_nginx_configuration;
        let content = match selection {
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(
                    div().child(section_label("Baseline 类型", tokens)).child(
                        div().mt_2().flex().gap_2().children(
                            NginxSiteKind::ALL.into_iter().map(|option| {
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-kind-{option:?}"
                                )))
                                .label(option.label())
                                .small()
                                .selected(self.model.kind == option)
                                .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                    page.select_kind(option, window, cx)
                                }))
                            }),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                .child(input_field(
                    "域名",
                    NginxServerNames::meaning(),
                    &self.server_name_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    "监听端口",
                    "范围 1 - 65535。",
                    &self.listen_port_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(match self.model.kind {
                    NginxSiteKind::Static => input_field(
                        "站点目录",
                        "绝对 POSIX 路径；拒绝遍历、空格和 Nginx 元字符。",
                        &self.static_root_input,
                        tokens,
                    ),
                    NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy => input_field(
                        "上游地址",
                        "仅支持 http/https origin，例如 http://127.0.0.1:3000。",
                        &self.upstream_input,
                        tokens,
                    ),
                })
                .into_any_element(),
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    "禁止访问路径",
                    "规范绝对 URI，例如 /admin；可选择只匹配当前路径或同时覆盖子路径。",
                    &self.forbidden_path_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(
                    div().child(section_label("匹配范围", tokens)).child(
                        div().mt_2().flex().gap_2().children(
                            NginxForbiddenPathScope::ALL.into_iter().map(|scope| {
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-forbidden-scope-{scope:?}"
                                )))
                                .label(scope.label())
                                .small()
                                .selected(self.model.forbidden_path_scope == scope)
                                .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                    page.select_forbidden_path_scope(scope, cx)
                                }))
                            }),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp) => div()
                .child(input_field(
                    "允许的直接 TCP 来源 IP / CIDR",
                    "匹配直接连接 Nginx 的 peer；生成 satisfy all、allow 与 deny all，不读取客户端提供的 X-Forwarded-For。若前面有代理，本项看到的是该代理地址。",
                    &self.allowed_source_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => div()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child(format!(
                    "{} 固定使用已审查模板，无额外自由文本参数；它会与 Baseline 及其他已选配置一起生成。",
                    feature.summary()
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "编辑 Baseline".to_owned(),
                        NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        match selection {
                            NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                        },
                        |header, feature| {
                            let remove_selector =
                                format!("config-generator-remove-{}", feature.key());
                            let debug_selector = remove_selector.clone();
                            header.child(
                                div()
                                    .id(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-remove-hit-{}",
                                        feature.key()
                                    )))
                                    .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                                    .on_mouse_down(
                                        MouseButton::Left,
                                        cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                            cx.stop_propagation();
                                            page.remove_nginx_configuration(feature, cx)
                                        }),
                                    )
                                    .child(
                                        crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(remove_selector))
                                            .label("移除这项配置")
                                            .small()
                                            .disabled(!self.model.nginx_features.contains(&feature))
                                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                                cx.stop_propagation();
                                                page.remove_nginx_configuration(feature, cx)
                                            })),
                                    ),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_apache_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            ApacheSiteFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| apache_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.apache_features.len();
        let kind = self.model.apache_kind;

        div()
            .id("config-generator-apache-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-apache-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-apache-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-apache-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .font_semibold()
                            .child(format!("Baseline 已加载：Apache HTTPD {}", kind.label())),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(92.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(46.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_apache_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled, applicable) = match selection {
                            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!("{} Baseline", kind.label()),
                                "提供 VirtualHost、域名及静态站点或反向代理主流程；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "站点".to_owned(),
                                true,
                                true,
                            ),
                            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.scope().to_owned(),
                                self.model.apache_features.contains(&feature),
                                feature.supports_kind(kind),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-apache-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = if !applicable {
                            "不适用"
                        } else if selection == ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline {
                            "编辑"
                        } else if enabled {
                            "已添加"
                        } else {
                            "选择"
                        };

                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(92.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(46.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-apache-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .disabled(!applicable)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_apache_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_apache_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终包含")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_apache_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_apache_configuration;
        let content = match selection {
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_3()
                    .child(
                        div().child(section_label("Baseline 类型", tokens)).child(
                            div().mt_2().flex().gap_2().children(
                                ApacheSiteKind::ALL.into_iter().map(|option| {
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-apache-kind-{option:?}"
                                    )))
                                    .label(option.label())
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(self.model.apache_kind == option)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.select_apache_kind(option, window, cx)
                                    }))
                                }),
                            ),
                        ),
                    )
                    .child(div().text_xs().text_color(tokens.muted).child(format!(
                                "Baseline 必需模块：{}",
                                self.model
                                    .apache_kind
                                    .baseline_required_modules()
                                    .join("、")
                            )))
                    .child(input_field(
                        "域名",
                        "接受一个精确 DNS 名称；保存前请按实际站点修改。",
                        &self.server_name_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(input_field(
                        "VirtualHost 端口",
                        "范围 1 - 65535；生成文件不重复声明 Listen，请在主配置确认监听。",
                        &self.listen_port_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(match self.model.apache_kind {
                        ApacheSiteKind::Static => input_field(
                            "站点目录",
                            "绝对 POSIX 路径；拒绝遍历、空格和 Apache 配置元字符。",
                            &self.static_root_input,
                            tokens,
                        ),
                        ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy => input_field(
                            "上游地址",
                            "首版只支持 http:// origin；HTTPS 上游需要完整 CA 校验模型后再开放。",
                            &self.upstream_input,
                            tokens,
                        ),
                    })
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    "禁止访问路径",
                    "规范绝对 URI，例如 /admin；可选择只匹配当前路径或同时覆盖子路径。",
                    &self.forbidden_path_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(div().child(section_label("匹配范围", tokens)).child(
                    div().mt_2().flex().gap_2().children(
                        ApacheForbiddenPathScope::ALL.into_iter().map(|scope| {
                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                "config-generator-apache-forbidden-scope-{scope:?}"
                            )))
                            .label(scope.label())
                            .small()
                            .selected(self.model.apache_forbidden_path_scope == scope)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(
                                move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                    page.select_apache_forbidden_path_scope(scope, cx)
                                },
                            ))
                        }),
                    ),
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp) => div()
                .child(input_field(
                    "允许的来源 IP / CIDR",
                    "必须填写一个明确的 IP 或 CIDR；会在每个生成入口重复写入 Require ip。",
                    &self.allowed_source_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                let modules = feature.required_modules();
                let module_note = if modules.is_empty() {
                    "仅使用 Apache core 指令".to_owned()
                } else {
                    format!("需要目标加载：{}", modules.join("、"))
                };
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!(
                        "{} 固定使用已审查模板，无额外自由文本参数；它会与 Baseline 及其他已选配置一起生成。",
                        feature.summary()
                    ))
                    .child(module_note)
                    .into_any_element()
            }
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-apache-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-apache-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "编辑 Baseline".to_owned(),
                        ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        match selection {
                            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                        },
                        |header, feature| {
                            header.child(
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-remove-apache-{}",
                                    feature.key()
                                )))
                                .label("移除这项配置")
                                .small()
                                .disabled(!self.model.apache_features.contains(&feature))
                                .on_click(cx.listener(
                                    move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.remove_apache_configuration(feature, cx)
                                    },
                                )),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_mysql_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            MySqlConfigFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| mysql_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.mysql_features.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-mysql-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mysql-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-mysql-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mysql-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .font_semibold()
                            .child("Baseline 已加载：MySQL 8.4"),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(110.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(54.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_mysql_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled) = match selection {
                            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                "MySQL 8.4 Baseline".to_owned(),
                                "固定 [mysqld]、经典协议本机监听、3306、X Plugin 关闭、LOCAL 导入关闭及 FILE 日志输出；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "实例".to_owned(),
                                true,
                            ),
                            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.scope().to_owned(),
                                self.model.mysql_features.contains(&feature),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-mysql-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = if selection == MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline {
                            "查看"
                        } else if enabled {
                            "已添加"
                        } else {
                            "选择"
                        };
                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(110.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(54.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-mysql-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_mysql_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_mysql_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终包含")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_mysql_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_mysql_configuration;
        let content = match selection {
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child("版本固定为 MySQL 8.4.x；Baseline 不允许移除或自由改写。")
                .child("[mysqld] · bind_address = 127.0.0.1 · port = 3306")
                .child("mysqlx = OFF（关闭 X Plugin 的 33060 监听）")
                .child("local_infile = OFF · log_output = FILE")
                .child("保存后请使用匹配版本的 mysqld --defaults-file=<path> --validate-config 校验。")
                .into_any_element(),
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::RemoteListener) => {
                input_field(
                    "监听 IP",
                    "必须是规范 IPv4 或 IPv6 地址；启用后替换 Baseline 的 127.0.0.1。",
                    &self.mysql_remote_listener_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections) => {
                input_field(
                    "最大连接数",
                    "允许范围 1 - 100000；生成 max_connections。",
                    &self.mysql_max_connections_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::ConnectTimeout) => {
                input_field(
                    "握手超时（秒）",
                    "允许范围 2 - 31536000；生成 connect_timeout。",
                    &self.mysql_connect_timeout_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnectErrors) => {
                input_field(
                    "连接错误阈值",
                    "允许范围 1 - 4294967295；生成 max_connect_errors。",
                    &self.mysql_max_connect_errors_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::SessionTimeouts) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    "非交互空闲超时（秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 2147483；生成 wait_timeout。",
                    &self.mysql_wait_timeout_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    "交互空闲超时（秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 31536000；生成 interactive_timeout。",
                    &self.mysql_interactive_timeout_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog) => input_field(
                "慢查询阈值（秒）",
                "允许范围 0 - 31536000；同时启用 slow_query_log 并生成 long_query_time。",
                &self.mysql_long_query_time_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::LogErrorVerbosity) => {
                input_field(
                    "错误日志详细级别",
                    "允许值 1 - 3；生成 log_error_verbosity。",
                    &self.mysql_log_error_verbosity_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging) => input_field(
                "server_id",
                "必须为非零 32 位整数；固定 log_bin=binlog。MySQL 8.4 默认 ROW，不写已弃用的 binlog_format。",
                &self.mysql_server_id_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention) => {
                input_field(
                    "二进制日志保留时间（秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 4294967295；选择此项会同时加入二进制日志配置。",
                    &self.mysql_binlog_retention_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child(format!(
                    "{} 固定使用已审查模板，无额外自由文本参数；它会与 Baseline 及其他已选配置一起生成。",
                    feature.summary()
                ))
                .child(format!("生成键：{}", feature.owned_keys().join("、")))
                .into_any_element(),
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-mysql-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mysql-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "查看 Baseline".to_owned(),
                        MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        match selection {
                            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                            MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                        },
                        |header, feature| {
                            header.child(
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-remove-mysql-{}",
                                    feature.key()
                                )))
                                .label("移除这项配置")
                                .small()
                                .disabled(!self.model.mysql_features.contains(&feature))
                                .on_click(cx.listener(
                                    move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.remove_mysql_configuration(feature, cx)
                                    },
                                )),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_postgresql_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| postgresql_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.postgresql_features.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-postgresql-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-postgresql-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-postgresql-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(format!(
                        "Baseline 已加载：PostgreSQL {} 新建空集群",
                        self.model.postgresql_version.label()
                    )))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(126.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("激活"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_postgresql_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, activation, enabled) = match selection {
                            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!(
                                    "PostgreSQL {} Baseline",
                                    self.model.postgresql_version.label()
                                ),
                                "固定回环监听、SCRAM 新密码、WAL/提交耐久性、统计与 autovacuum；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "新集群".to_owned(),
                                true,
                            ),
                            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.activation().to_owned(),
                                self.model.postgresql_features.contains(&feature),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-postgresql-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = if selection
                            == PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline
                        {
                            "查看"
                        } else if enabled {
                            "已添加"
                        } else {
                            "选择"
                        };
                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(126.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(78.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(activation),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-postgresql-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_postgresql_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_postgresql_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终包含")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_postgresql_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_postgresql_configuration;
        let version = self.model.postgresql_version;
        let content = match selection {
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(
                    div()
                        .rounded_md()
                        .border_1()
                        .border_color(tokens.warning)
                        .p_2()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child("仅生成供新建空集群手工 include 的不完整片段；绝不能覆盖既有或含数据集群。"),
                )
                .child(div().child(section_label("PostgreSQL major（必须显式选择）", tokens)).child(
                    div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                        PostgreSqlVersion::ALL.into_iter().map(|candidate| {
                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                "config-generator-postgresql-version-{candidate:?}"
                            )))
                            .label(candidate.label())
                            .small()
                            .selected(version == candidate)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                page.select_postgresql_version(candidate, window, cx)
                            }))
                        }),
                    ),
                ))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(version.support_notice()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(version.risk()),
                )
                .child(input_field(
                    "TCP 端口",
                    "允许范围 1 - 65535；监听仍固定 localhost，真实准入必须独立审阅 pg_hba.conf。",
                    &self.postgresql_port_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity,
            ) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    "max_connections",
                    "允许范围 1 - 10000；必须结合目标内存、连接池与内核限制 sizing。",
                    &self.postgresql_max_connections_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    "superuser_reserved_connections",
                    "允许范围 0 - 10000；与普通保留槽之和必须小于 max_connections。",
                    &self.postgresql_superuser_reserved_connections_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .when(version.supports_reserved_connections(), |editor| {
                    editor.child(input_field(
                        "reserved_connections（PostgreSQL 16+）",
                        "允许范围 0 - 10000；14/15 不存在此参数，切换到旧 major 会自动归零。",
                        &self.postgresql_reserved_connections_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                })
                .when(!version.supports_reserved_connections(), |editor| {
                    editor.child(
                        div()
                            .rounded_md()
                            .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            .p_2()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child("PostgreSQL 14/15 不支持 reserved_connections；UI 固定为 0 且生成器不会输出该键。"),
                    )
                })
                .into_any_element(),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AuthenticationTimeout,
            ) => input_field(
                "authentication_timeout（秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 3600；过短会拒绝高延迟或外部认证较慢的合法客户端。",
                &self.postgresql_authentication_timeout_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleInTransactionGuard,
            ) => input_field(
                "idle_in_transaction_session_timeout（秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 604800；超时会断开会话并回滚未提交事务。",
                &self.postgresql_idle_in_transaction_timeout_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleSessionGuard,
            ) => input_field(
                "idle_session_timeout（秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 604800；事务外空闲会话会被主动断开，连接池必须先验证重连行为。",
                &self.postgresql_idle_session_timeout_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLimit,
            ) => input_field(
                "temp_file_limit（MiB）",
                "允许范围 1 - 1048576；按 PostgreSQL 进程计算且不覆盖显式临时表。",
                &self.postgresql_temporary_file_limit_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::LockWaitLogging,
            ) => input_field(
                "deadlock_timeout / log_lock_waits（毫秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 3600000；生成器会成组设置 deadlock_timeout 与 log_lock_waits，过低会增加死锁检查和日志开销。",
                &self.postgresql_lock_wait_threshold_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AutovacuumLogging,
            ) => input_field(
                "log_autovacuum_min_duration（毫秒）",
                "允许范围 0 - 86400000；0 会记录全部 autovacuum 动作，繁忙实例可能产生大量日志。",
                &self.postgresql_autovacuum_log_threshold_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLogging,
            ) => input_field(
                "log_temp_files（KiB）",
                "允许范围 0 - 1073741824；0 会记录全部临时文件，日志可能包含文件路径和大小。",
                &self.postgresql_temporary_file_log_threshold_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::WalCompression) => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(section_label("WAL 全页镜像压缩算法", tokens))
                    .child(div().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                        PostgreSqlWalCompression::ALL.into_iter().map(|compression| {
                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                "config-generator-postgresql-wal-compression-{compression:?}"
                            )))
                            .label(compression.label())
                            .small()
                            .selected(self.model.postgresql_wal_compression == compression)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                page.select_postgresql_wal_compression(compression, cx)
                            }))
                        }),
                    ))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!(
                                "PostgreSQL {} 将生成 wal_compression = {}；14 使用 on，15–18 使用语义相同的 pglz。",
                                version.label(),
                                self.model.postgresql_wal_compression.config_value(version)
                            )),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child("当前仅开放所有目标构建都具备的内建 pglz；lz4/zstd 在能力清单未知时失败关闭。"),
                    )
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child(feature.summary())
                .child(format!("激活方式：{}", feature.activation()))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(feature.risk()),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-postgresql-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => {
                            "查看 Baseline 与版本".to_owned()
                        }
                        PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        match selection {
                            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                        },
                        |header, feature| {
                            header.child(
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-remove-postgresql-{}",
                                    feature.key()
                                )))
                                .label("移除这项配置")
                                .small()
