                                .disabled(!self.model.postgresql_features.contains(&feature))
                                .on_click(cx.listener(
                                    move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.remove_postgresql_configuration(feature, cx)
                                    },
                                )),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .when_some(
                match selection {
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                },
                |editor, feature| {
                    let compatible = feature.supports_version(version);
                    editor.child(
                        div()
                            .id("config-generator-postgresql-feature-version-compatibility")
                            .debug_selector(|| {
                                "config-generator-postgresql-feature-version-compatibility"
                                    .to_owned()
                            })
                            .rounded_md()
                            .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            .p_2()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(if compatible {
                                tokens.muted
                            } else {
                                tokens.warning
                            })
                            .child(format!(
                                "版本兼容：PostgreSQL {} {}",
                                version.label(),
                                if compatible {
                                    "已支持（该项覆盖 14–18）"
                                } else {
                                    "不支持，生成已失败关闭"
                                }
                            )),
                    )
                },
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_mariadb_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            MariaDbConfigFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| mariadb_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.mariadb_features.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-mariadb-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mariadb-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-mariadb-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mariadb-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(format!(
                        "Baseline 已加载：MariaDB {} Community LTS 新建空实例",
                        self.model.mariadb_version.label()
                    )))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(126.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(72.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_mariadb_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled) = match selection {
                            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!(
                                    "MariaDB {} Baseline",
                                    self.model.mariadb_version.label()
                                ),
                                "固定回环监听、显式端口、关闭 LOCAL 导入，并保留新建空实例和版本校验边界；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "实例".to_owned(),
                                true,
                            ),
                            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.scope().to_owned(),
                                self.model.mariadb_features.contains(&feature),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-mariadb-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = if selection == MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline {
                            "查看"
                        } else if enabled {
                            "已添加"
                        } else {
                            "选择"
                        };
                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(126.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(72.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-mariadb-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_mariadb_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_mariadb_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!("共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终包含")),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_mariadb_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_mariadb_configuration;
        let version = self.model.mariadb_version;
        let content = match selection {
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(
                    div()
                        .rounded_md()
                        .border_1()
                        .border_color(tokens.warning)
                        .p_2()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child("仅生成新建空实例的 review-only option fragment；不能替换既有实例配置、迁移数据或直接部署。"),
                )
                .child(div().child(section_label("MariaDB Community LTS（必须显式选择）", tokens)).child(
                    div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                        MariaDbVersion::ALL.into_iter().map(|candidate| {
                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                "config-generator-mariadb-version-{candidate:?}"
                            )))
                            .label(candidate.label())
                            .small()
                            .selected(version == candidate)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                page.select_mariadb_version(candidate, cx)
                            }))
                        }),
                    ),
                ))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(version.support_notice()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(version.risk()),
                )
                .child(input_field(
                    "TCP 端口",
                    "允许范围 1 - 65535；监听仍固定 127.0.0.1，用户 Host/GRANT 与 TLS 必须独立审阅。",
                    &self.mariadb_port_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity) => {
                input_field(
                    "max_connections",
                    "允许范围 10 - 100000；必须结合目标内存、线程、文件描述符与连接池 sizing。",
                    &self.mariadb_max_connections_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::HandshakeTimeout) => {
                input_field(
                    "connect_timeout（秒）",
                    "允许范围 2 - 31536000；过短会拒绝高延迟或认证较慢的合法客户端。",
                    &self.mariadb_connect_timeout_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::SessionIdleTimeouts) => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_3()
                    .child(input_field(
                        "wait_timeout（秒）",
                        "允许范围 1 - 31536000；连接池必须能检测并重建被关闭的连接。",
                        &self.mariadb_wait_timeout_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(input_field(
                        "interactive_timeout（秒）",
                        "允许范围 1 - 31536000；与非交互超时分别生成。",
                        &self.mariadb_interactive_timeout_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::HostErrorThreshold) => {
                input_field(
                    "max_connect_errors",
                    "允许范围 1 - 4294967295；过低可能让攻击流量阻断合法来源。",
                    &self.mariadb_max_connect_errors_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::MaximumPacketSize) => {
                input_field(
                    "max_allowed_packet（MiB）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 1024；过低会使合法大行、BLOB 或复制事件失败。",
                    &self.mariadb_max_allowed_packet_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog) => {
                input_field(
                    "log_slow_query_time（秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 3600；日志可能包含 SQL、字面量和敏感数据。",
                    &self.mariadb_slow_query_time_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging) => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(input_field(
                        "server_id",
                        "必须是非零 32 位整数且在复制拓扑中唯一；固定传统文件式 log_bin，不配置复制/GTID。",
                        &self.mariadb_server_id_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child(MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging.version_notice(version)),
                    )
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention) => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(input_field(
                        "binlog_expire_logs_seconds",
                        "允许范围 1 - 8553600；选择时自动加入“启用传统文件式二进制日志”。",
                        &self.mariadb_binlog_retention_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child("依赖：BinaryLogging。移除 BinaryLogging 会同时移除保留时间和每提交同步。"),
                    )
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation,
            ) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .child(section_label("字符集 / 排序规则闭合配置", tokens))
                .child(div().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                    MariaDbCharacterProfile::ALL.into_iter().map(|profile| {
                        crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                            "config-generator-mariadb-character-profile-{profile:?}"
                        )))
                        .label(profile.label())
                        .small()
                        .selected(self.model.mariadb_character_profile == profile)
                        .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                            page.select_mariadb_character_profile(profile, cx)
                        }))
                    }),
                ))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(self.model.mariadb_character_profile.summary()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(
                            MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation
                                .version_notice(version),
                        ),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(self.model.mariadb_character_profile.risk()),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbLockWaitTimeout,
            ) => input_field(
                "innodb_lock_wait_timeout（秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 3600；超时只让等待语句失败，应用必须正确回滚或重试事务。",
                &self.mariadb_innodb_lock_wait_timeout_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child(feature.summary())
                .child(format!("生成键：{}", feature.owned_keys().join("、")))
                .when_some(feature.required_feature(), |editor, required| {
                    editor.child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child(format!(
                                "依赖：{}；选择本项会自动加入依赖，移除依赖会级联移除本项。",
                                required.title()
                            )),
                    )
                })
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(feature.version_notice(version)),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(feature.risk()),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-mariadb-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-mariadb-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline => {
                            "查看 Baseline 与版本".to_owned()
                        }
                        MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        match selection {
                            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
                            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some(feature),
                        },
                        |header, feature| {
                            header.child(
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-remove-mariadb-{}",
                                    feature.key()
                                )))
                                .label("移除这项配置")
                                .small()
                                .disabled(!self.model.mariadb_features.contains(&feature))
                                .on_click(cx.listener(
                                    move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.remove_mariadb_configuration(feature, cx)
                                    },
                                )),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_redis_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            RedisConfigFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| redis_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.redis_features.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-redis-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-redis-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-redis-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-redis-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(format!(
                        "Baseline 已加载：Redis {} 新建空实例",
                        self.model.redis_version.label()
                    )))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(110.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(54.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_redis_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled) = match selection {
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!("Redis {} Baseline", self.model.redis_version.label()),
                                "固定回环绑定、protected-mode、端口、数据目录与新建空实例安全边界；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "实例".to_owned(),
                                true,
                            ),
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.scope().to_owned(),
                                self.model.redis_features.contains(&feature),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-redis-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = match selection {
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "查看",
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit) => {
                                "必需"
                            }
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(_) if enabled => "已添加",
                            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(_) => "选择",
                        };
                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(110.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(54.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-redis-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_redis_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_redis_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!(
                        "共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline、maxmemory 及至少一种持久化始终保留"
                    )),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_redis_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_redis_configuration;
        let content = match selection {
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(
                    div()
                        .rounded_md()
                        .border_1()
                        .border_color(tokens.warning)
                        .p_2()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child("仅用于新建空 Redis standalone 实例；不能用于既有含数据实例的 AOF/RDB 迁移，也不是部署授权。"),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(REDIS_EXISTING_INSTANCE_MIGRATION_DEFERRED_REASON),
                )
                .child(
                    div().child(section_label("Redis 版本", tokens)).child(
                        div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                            RedisVersion::ALL.into_iter().map(|version| {
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-redis-version-{version:?}"
                                )))
                                .label(version.label())
                                .small()
                                .selected(self.model.redis_version == version)
                                .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                    page.select_redis_version(version, cx)
                                }))
                            }),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                .child(
                    div().child(section_label("监听范围", tokens)).child(
                        div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                            RedisBindProfile::ALL.into_iter().map(|profile| {
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-redis-bind-{profile:?}"
                                )))
                                .label(profile.label())
                                .small()
                                .selected(self.model.redis_bind_profile == profile)
                                .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                    page.select_redis_bind_profile(profile, cx)
                                }))
                            }),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                .child(input_field(
                    "数据目录（dir）",
                    "必须是专用绝对 POSIX 路径；首次启动前验证属主、权限、容量与备份策略。",
                    &self.redis_data_directory_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    "TCP 端口",
                    "允许范围 1 - 65535；仍固定 protected-mode yes 且只允许回环绑定。",
                    &self.redis_port_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients) => input_field(
                "最大客户端数（maxclients）",
                "允许范围 1 - 1000000；实际仍受目标 OS 文件描述符与 Redis 预留描述符限制。",
                &self.redis_max_clients_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout) => {
                input_field(
                    "空闲客户端超时（秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 31536000；移除此项会显式生成 timeout 0。",
                    &self.redis_idle_timeout_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive) => input_field(
                "TCP Keepalive（秒）",
                "允许范围 1 - 86400；移除此项会显式生成 tcp-keepalive 0。",
                &self.redis_tcp_keepalive_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit) => input_field(
                "最大内存（MiB）",
                "必须是非零整数；需要额外为 Redis 开销、持久化缓冲与操作系统保留内存，不能移除。",
                &self.redis_maxmemory_input,
                tokens,
            ),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .child(section_label("淘汰策略", tokens))
                .child(div().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                    RedisEvictionPolicy::ALL.into_iter().map(|policy| {
                        crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                            "config-generator-redis-eviction-{policy:?}"
                        )))
                        .label(policy.label())
                        .small()
                        .selected(self.model.redis_eviction_policy == policy)
                        .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                            page.select_redis_eviction_policy(policy, cx)
                        }))
                    }),
                ))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(self.model.redis_eviction_policy.summary()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(self.model.redis_eviction_policy.risk()),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .child(section_label("AOF fsync 策略", tokens))
                .child(div().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                    RedisAppendFsync::ALL.into_iter().map(|policy| {
                        crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                            "config-generator-redis-fsync-{policy:?}"
                        )))
                        .label(policy.label())
                        .small()
                        .selected(self.model.redis_append_fsync == policy)
                        .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                            page.select_redis_append_fsync(policy, cx)
                        }))
                    }),
                ))
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.muted)
                        .child(self.model.redis_append_fsync.summary()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child(self.model.redis_append_fsync.risk()),
                )
                .child(
                    div()
                        .text_xs()
                        .text_color(tokens.warning)
                        .child("只为新建空实例配置；既有实例必须走实时迁移、备份、回滚与数据验证流程。"),
                )
                .into_any_element(),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .text_color(tokens.muted)
                .child("使用三档 save 计划，并启用后台保存错误保护、压缩和校验和。")
                .child("RDB fork、磁盘空间、写时复制内存和恢复点间隔必须在真实目标上验证。")
                .child("只为新建空实例配置；既有实例不能靠静态替换 redis.conf 迁移数据。")
                .into_any_element(),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    "慢命令阈值（微秒）",
                    "允许范围 0 - 86400000000；只统计命令执行时间，不包含网络 I/O。",
                    &self.redis_slowlog_threshold_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    "Slowlog 最大条目数",
                    "允许范围 1 - 1000000；队列在内存中，过大将增加常驻内存。",
                    &self.redis_slowlog_length_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor) => {
                input_field(
                    "延迟监控阈值（毫秒）",
                    "允许范围 1 - 60000；启用后记录超过阈值的 Redis 内部延迟事件。",
                    &self.redis_latency_threshold_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
        };
        let removable_feature = match selection {
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => Some((
                feature,
                self.redis_feature_removable(feature),
                self.model.redis_features.contains(&feature),
            )),
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-redis-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-redis-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "查看 Baseline".to_owned(),
                        RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(
                        removable_feature,
                        |header, (feature, removable, enabled)| {
                            header.child(
                                crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                    "config-generator-remove-redis-{}",
                                    feature.key()
                                )))
                                .label(if feature == RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit {
                                    "Baseline 必需"
                                } else if !removable {
                                    "至少保留一种持久化"
                                } else {
                                    "移除这项配置"
                                })
                                .small()
                                .disabled(!enabled || !removable)
                                .on_click(cx.listener(
                                    move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.remove_redis_configuration(feature, cx)
                                    },
                                )),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_tomcat_configuration_table(
        &self,
        query: &str,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let mut configurations = vec![TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline];
        configurations.extend(
            TomcatConfigFeature::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .map(TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature),
        );
        configurations.retain(|selection| tomcat_configuration_matches(*selection, query));
        let visible_count = configurations.len();
        let enabled_count = self.model.tomcat_features.len();

        div()
            .id("config-generator-tomcat-configuration-table")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-tomcat-configuration-table".to_owned())
            .border_1()
            .border_color(tokens.border)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-tomcat-baseline-status")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-tomcat-baseline-status".to_owned())
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(format!(
                        "Baseline 已加载：{} · {} · Java {}+ · {}",
                        self.model.tomcat_version.label(),
                        self.model.tomcat_version.servlet_specification(),
                        self.model.tomcat_version.minimum_java(),
                        self.model.tomcat_version.servlet_api_namespace(),
                    )))
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .text_color(tokens.muted)
                            .child(format!("· 已叠加 {enabled_count} 项配置")),
                    ),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .h(px(34.0))
                    .px_2()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .border_b_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                    .text_xs()
                    .font_semibold()
                    .child(div().w(px(28.0)).flex_none().child("序号"))
                    .child(div().w(px(126.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("配置名称"))
                    .child(div().flex_1().min_w_0().truncate().child("简要说明"))
                    .child(div().w(px(92.0)).flex_none().truncate().child("工件范围"))
                    .child(div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().text_right().child("操作")),
            )
            .children(
                configurations
                    .into_iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(index, selection)| {
                        let active = self.model.active_tomcat_configuration == selection;
                        let (title, summary, scope, enabled) = match selection {
                            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => (
                                format!("{} Baseline", self.model.tomcat_version.label()),
                                "固定三工件闭合结构、关闭 shutdown 端口、回环 NIO、关闭 TRACE 与版本化 web.xml；始终保留。"
                                    .to_owned(),
                                "三工件".to_owned(),
                                true,
                            ),
                            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => (
                                feature.title().to_owned(),
                                feature.summary().to_owned(),
                                feature.artifact().relative_path().to_owned(),
                                self.model.tomcat_features.contains(&feature),
                            ),
                        };
                        let selector = format!(
                            "config-generator-tomcat-configuration-row-{}",
                            selection.key()
                        );
                        let debug_selector = selector.clone();
                        let action_label = match selection {
                            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "查看",
                            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(_) if enabled => "已添加",
                            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(_) => "选择",
                        };
                        let row = div()
                            .id(SharedString::from(selector))
                            .debug_selector(move || debug_selector.clone())
                            .min_h(px(48.0))
                            .px_2()
                            .py_2()
                            .flex()
                            .items_center()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .when(active, |row| {
                                row.border_l_2()
                                    .border_color(tokens.accent)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            })
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(28.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(126.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .font_semibold()
                                    .child(format!("{} {title}", if enabled { "✓" } else { "○" })),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_w_0()
                                    .pr_2()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(summary),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .w(px(92.0))
                                    .flex_none()
                                    .truncate()
                                    .text_xs()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(scope),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div().w(px(78.0)).flex_none().flex().justify_end().child(
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-select-tomcat-{}",
                                        selection.key()
                                    )))
                                    .label(action_label)
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(enabled && active)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.open_tomcat_configuration(selection, window, cx)
                                    })),
                                ),
                            );

                        div().flex().flex_col().child(row).when(active, |group| {
                            group.child(self.render_tomcat_configuration_editor(tokens, cx))
                        })
                    }),
            )
            .child(
                div()
                    .px_3()
                    .py_2()
                    .text_xs()
                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                    .child(format!(
                        "共 {visible_count} 项 · Baseline 始终保留 · GET/HEAD allow-list 与全应用 403 原子互斥"
                    )),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_tomcat_configuration_editor(
        &self,
        tokens: ThemeTokens,
        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
    ) -> AnyElement {
        let selection = self.model.active_tomcat_configuration;
        let content = match selection {
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => {
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .gap_3()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .rounded_md()
                            .border_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.warning)
                            .p_2()
                            .text_xs()
                            .text_color(tokens.warning)
                            .child(TOMCAT_BUNDLE_SCOPE_NOTICE),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .child(section_label("Tomcat 版本与 Servlet schema", tokens))
                            .child(div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                                TomcatVersionFamily::ALL.into_iter().map(|version| {
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-tomcat-version-{version:?}"
                                    )))
                                    .label(format!(
                                        "{} · {} · Java {}+ · {}",
                                        version.label(),
                                        version.servlet_specification(),
                                        version.minimum_java(),
                                        version.servlet_api_namespace(),
                                    ))
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(self.model.tomcat_version == version)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.select_tomcat_version(version, cx)
                                    }))
                                }),
                            )),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div().child(section_label("回环监听地址", tokens)).child(
                            div().mt_2().flex().flex_wrap().gap_2().children(
                                [
                                    TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv4Loopback,
                                    TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv6Loopback,
                                ]
                                .into_iter()
                                .map(|address| {
                                    crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                        "config-generator-tomcat-address-{address:?}"
                                    )))
                                    .label(address.label())
                                    .small()
                                    .selected(self.model.tomcat_connector_address == address)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |page, _, _, cx| {
                                        page.select_tomcat_connector_address(address, cx)
                                    }))
                                }),
                            ),
                        ),
                    )
                    .child(input_field(
                        "ROOT 应用名称",
                        "仅写入新 ROOT 应用的显示名称；不会导入、覆盖或部署现有应用。",
                        &self.tomcat_application_name_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(input_field(
                        TomcatConnectorField::Port.title(),
                        TomcatConnectorField::Port.summary(),
                        &self.tomcat_port_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(input_field(
                        TomcatConnectorField::ConnectionTimeout.title(),
                        TomcatConnectorField::ConnectionTimeout.summary(),
                        &self.tomcat_connection_timeout_input,
                        tokens,
                    ))
                    .child(section_label("不可移除的 Baseline 边界", tokens))
                    .children(TomcatBaselineInvariant::ALL.into_iter().map(|invariant| {
                        div()
                            .rounded_md()
                            .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                            .p_2()
                            .text_xs()
                            .child(div().font_semibold().child(format!(
                                "{} · {}",
                                invariant.title(),
                                invariant.scope()
                            )))
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .mt_1()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child(invariant.summary()),
                            )
                    }))
                    .child(section_label("明确 deferred 的能力", tokens))
                    .children(TomcatDeferredCapability::ALL.into_iter().map(|capability| {
                        div()
                            .rounded_md()
                            .border_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.warning)
                            .p_2()
                            .text_xs()
                            .child(div().font_semibold().child(capability.key()))
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .mt_1()
                                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                    .child(capability.reason()),
                            )
                    }))
                    .into_any_element()
            }
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::ConnectorLimits) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_3()
                .child(input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxThreads.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxThreads.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_max_threads_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::AcceptCount.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::AcceptCount.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_accept_count_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxConnections.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxConnections.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_max_connections_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxPostSize.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxPostSize.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_max_post_size_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .child(input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxSwallowSize.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::MaxSwallowSize.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_max_swallow_size_input,
                    tokens,
                ))
                .into_any_element(),
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression) => {
                input_field(
                    TomcatConnectorField::CompressionMinSize.title(),
                    TomcatConnectorField::CompressionMinSize.summary(),
                    &self.tomcat_compression_min_size_input,
                    tokens,
                )
            }
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => div()
                .flex()
                .flex_col()
                .gap_2()
                .text_xs()
                .child(div().font_semibold().child(feature.title()))
                .child(div().text_color(tokens.muted).child(feature.summary()))
                .child(div().text_color(tokens.muted).child(format!(
                    "生成位置：{} · {}",
                    feature.artifact().relative_path(),
                    feature.scope()
                )))
                .when(
                    matches!(
                        feature,
                        TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly
                            | TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests
                    ),
                    |content| {
                        content.child(
                            div()
                                .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                .child("这两项互斥；选择当前项会在同一次状态变更中移除另一项。"),
                        )
                    },
                )
                .into_any_element(),
        };
        let removable_feature = match selection {
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => None,
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                Some((feature, self.model.tomcat_features.contains(&feature)))
            }
        };

        div()
            .id("config-generator-tomcat-configuration-editor")
            .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-tomcat-configuration-editor".to_owned())
            .border_l_2()
            .border_b_1()
            .border_color(tokens.accent)
            .bg(tokens.surface)
            .p_3()
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_3()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex()
                    .items_center()
                    .gap_2()
                    .child(div().font_semibold().child(match selection {
                        TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline => "查看 Baseline".to_owned(),
                        TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(feature) => {
                            format!("配置：{}", feature.title())
                        }
                    }))
                    .child(div().flex_1())
                    .when_some(removable_feature, |header, (feature, enabled)| {
                        header.child(
                            crate::buttons::button(SharedString::from(format!(
                                "config-generator-remove-tomcat-{}",
                                feature.key()
                            )))
                            .label("移除这项配置")
                            .small()
                            .disabled(!enabled)
                            .on_click(cx.listener(
                                move |page, _, _, cx| page.remove_tomcat_configuration(feature, cx),
                            )),
                        )
                    }),
            )
            .child(content)
            .into_any_element()
    }

    fn render_quick_workspace(&self, tokens: ThemeTokens, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> AnyElement {
        let kind = self.model.kind;
        let selected_goal_subject = self.model.selected_goal_subject;
        let database_assignment_recognized = self.database_assignment_recognized();
        let configuration_query =
            bounded_visual_search_query(self.nginx_goal_search_input.read(cx).value().as_ref());
        let goal_query = if database_assignment_recognized {
            String::new()
        } else if configuration_query.trim().is_empty() {
            selected_goal_subject.label().to_owned()
        } else {
            configuration_query.clone()
        };
        let goal_results = if database_assignment_recognized {
            None
        } else {
            search_configuration_goals(&goal_query).ok()
        };
        let simple_recipe_matches = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .into_iter()
            .flat_map(|results| results.matches())
            .filter_map(|goal| match *goal {
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(recipe)
                    if recipe.subject == selected_goal_subject
                        && recipe.action
                            != ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxForbiddenPath403 =>
                {
                    Some(recipe)
                }
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(_)
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { .. } => None,
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let forbidden_goal_matches = goal_results.as_ref().is_some_and(|results| {
            selected_goal_subject == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx)
                && results.matches().iter().any(|goal| {
                    matches!(
                        goal,
                        ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(recipe)
                            if recipe.subject == selected_goal_subject
                                && recipe.action
                                == ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxForbiddenPath403
                    )
                })
        });
        let database_bundle_goal_matches = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .into_iter()
            .flat_map(|results| results.matches())
            .filter_map(|matched| match *matched {
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal {
                    goal,
                    product,
                    version,
                } if selected_goal_subject == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(product) => {
                    Some((goal, product, version))
                }
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(_)
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { .. } => None,
            })
            .take(8)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let database_artifact_goal_matches = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .into_iter()
            .flat_map(|results| results.matches())
            .filter_map(|matched| match *matched {
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { artifact }
                    if selected_goal_subject
                        == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(artifact.product) =>
                {
                    Some(artifact)
                }
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(_)
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { .. } => None,
            })
            .take(24)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let database_goal_matches = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .into_iter()
            .flat_map(|results| results.matches())
            .filter_map(|goal| match *goal {
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter {
                    schema,
                    parameter_index,
                } if selected_goal_subject
                    == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(schema.binding.product) =>
                {
                    Some((schema, parameter_index))
                }
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(_)
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { .. } => None,
            })
            .take(24)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let unavailable_goal_subjects = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .into_iter()
            .flat_map(|results| results.matches())
            .filter_map(|goal| match *goal {
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { coverage, gap }
                    if coverage.subject == selected_goal_subject =>
                {
                    Some((coverage, gap))
                }
                ConfigurationGoalMatch::Recipe(_)
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseArtifact { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseGoal { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::DatabaseParameter { .. }
                | ConfigurationGoalMatch::UnavailableSubject { .. } => None,
            })
            .take(8)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let goal_results_truncated = goal_results
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|results| results.truncated());
        let no_goal_matches = !database_assignment_recognized
            && simple_recipe_matches.is_empty()
            && !forbidden_goal_matches
            && database_artifact_goal_matches.is_empty()
            && database_bundle_goal_matches.is_empty()
            && database_goal_matches.is_empty()
            && unavailable_goal_subjects.is_empty();
        let nginx_subject_selected =
            selected_goal_subject == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx);
        let apache_subject_selected = selected_goal_subject
            == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd);
        let mysql_subject_selected = selected_goal_subject
            == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql);
        let postgresql_subject_selected = selected_goal_subject
            == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql);
        let mariadb_subject_selected = selected_goal_subject
            == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb);
        let redis_subject_selected =
            selected_goal_subject == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis);
        let tomcat_subject_selected = selected_goal_subject
            == ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat);
        let quick_subject_selected = nginx_subject_selected
            || apache_subject_selected
            || mysql_subject_selected
            || postgresql_subject_selected
            || mariadb_subject_selected
            || redis_subject_selected
            || tomcat_subject_selected;
        let generated = quick_subject_selected
            .then(|| self.model.generated.clone())
            .flatten()
            .filter(|generated| generated.matches_subject(selected_goal_subject));
        let current_configuration_label = if nginx_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "{} Baseline + {} 项配置",
                kind.label(),
                self.model.nginx_features.len()
            )
        } else if apache_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "{} Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.apache_kind.label(),
                self.model.apache_features.len()
            )
        } else if mysql_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "MySQL 8.4 Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.mysql_features.len()
            )
        } else if postgresql_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "PostgreSQL {} 新建空集群 Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.postgresql_version.label(),
                self.model.postgresql_features.len()
            )
        } else if mariadb_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "MariaDB {} Community LTS 新建空实例 Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.mariadb_version.label(),
                self.model.mariadb_features.len()
            )
        } else if redis_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "Redis {} 新建空实例 Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.redis_version.label(),
                self.model.redis_features.len()
            )
        } else if tomcat_subject_selected {
            format!(
                "{} 新建空白环境三工件 Baseline + {} 项配置",
                self.model.tomcat_version.label(),
                self.model.tomcat_features.len()
            )
        } else {
            format!("{} · 请选择配置", selected_goal_subject.label())
        };
        let selected_feature_count = if nginx_subject_selected {
            self.model.nginx_features.len()
        } else if apache_subject_selected {
            self.model.apache_features.len()
        } else if mysql_subject_selected {
            self.model.mysql_features.len()
        } else if postgresql_subject_selected {
            self.model.postgresql_features.len()
        } else if mariadb_subject_selected {
            self.model.mariadb_features.len()
        } else if redis_subject_selected {
            self.model.redis_features.len()
        } else if tomcat_subject_selected {
            self.model.tomcat_features.len()
        } else {
            0
        };
        let saving = self.saving;
        div()
            .flex_1()
            .min_h_0()
            .flex()
            .items_start()
            .gap_2()
            .child(self.render_goal_product_catalog(tokens, cx))
            .child(
                div()
                    .id("config-generator-goal-configuration-list")
                    .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-goal-configuration-list".to_owned())
                    .flex_1()
                    .h_full()
                    .min_w_0()
                    .min_h_0()
                    .rounded_lg()
                    .border_1()
                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                    .bg(tokens.surface)
                    .flex()
                    .flex_col()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .flex_none()
                            .p_3()
                            .border_b_1()
                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                            .font_semibold()
                            .child("2. 选择配置"),
                    )
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .id("config-generator-goal-configuration-scroll")
                            .flex_1()
                            .min_h_0()
                            .overflow_y_scrollbar()
                            .p_3()
                            .flex()
                            .flex_col()
                            .gap_3()
                            .child(input_field(
                                "搜索配置",
                                "搜索配置名称、含义或目标关键词；结果限定为已审配方和静态参数依据。",
                                &self.nginx_goal_search_input,
                                tokens,
                            ))
                            .child(
                                div()
                            .flex()
                            .flex_wrap()
                            .items_center()
                            .gap_2()
                            .child(div().flex_1())
                            .child(
                                crate::buttons::button("config-generator-reset-goal-search")
                                    .label(if quick_subject_selected {
                                        "恢复默认配置"
                                    } else {
                                        "清空搜索"
                                    })
                                    .small()
                                    .disabled(self.saving || self.importing)
                                    .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                        page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx)
                                    })),
                            ),
                            )
                    .when(nginx_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_nginx_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(apache_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_apache_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(mysql_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_mysql_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(postgresql_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_postgresql_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(mariadb_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_mariadb_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(redis_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_redis_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when(tomcat_subject_selected, |panel| {
                        panel.child(self.render_tomcat_configuration_table(
                            &configuration_query,
                            tokens,
                            cx,
                        ))
                    })
                    .when_some(
                        self.render_database_assignment_card(tokens, cx),
                        |panel, card| panel.child(card),
                    )
                    .when(
                        !quick_subject_selected && !simple_recipe_matches.is_empty(),
                        |panel| {
                        panel.child(
                            div()
                                .id("config-generator-simple-goal-results")
                                .debug_selector(|| {
                                    "config-generator-simple-goal-results".to_owned()
                                })
                                .flex()
                                .flex_col()
                                .gap_2()
                                .children(simple_recipe_matches.into_iter().enumerate().map(
                                    |(recipe_index, recipe)| {
                                        let action = recipe.action;
                                        let query = goal_query.clone();
                                        div()
                                            .rounded_md()
                                            .border_1()
                                            .border_color(tokens.border)
                                            .bg(tokens.surface_alt)
                                            .p_3()
                                            .flex()
                                            .flex_col()
                                            .gap_2()
                                            .child(
                                                div()
                                                    .text_sm()
                                                    .font_semibold()
                                                    .child(format!(
                                                        "配置目标 · {}",
                                                        recipe.title
                                                    )),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                div()
                                                    .text_xs()
                                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                                    .child(format!(
                                                        "{} 预计：{}",
                                                        recipe.summary, recipe.expected_effect
                                                    )),
                                            )
