                                                    page.remove_selected_visual_node(window, cx)
                                                })),
                                            ),
                                    )
                                    .child(
                                        div()
                                            .flex()
                                            .flex_wrap()
                                            .gap_2()
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-root-directive",
                                                )
                                                .label(format!(
                                                    "根{}",
                                                    document_kind.statement_label()
                                                ))
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy
                                                        || !structured
                                                        || !allows_lossless_local_draft
                                                        || schema_catalog_selected,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Statement,
                                                        false,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-root-block",
                                                )
                                                .label(format!("根{}", document_kind.container_label()))
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy
                                                        || !structured
                                                        || !allows_lossless_local_draft
                                                        || !supports_containers
                                                        || schema_catalog_selected,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Container,
                                                        false,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-root-comment",
                                                )
                                                .label("根注释")
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy
                                                        || !structured
                                                        || !allows_lossless_local_draft
                                                        || schema_catalog_selected,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Comment,
                                                        false,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-child-directive",
                                                )
                                                .label(format!(
                                                    "子{}",
                                                    document_kind.statement_label()
                                                ))
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy || !selected_local_draft_container,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Statement,
                                                        true,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-child-block",
                                                )
                                                .label(format!("子{}", document_kind.container_label()))
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy
                                                        || !selected_local_draft_container
                                                        || !supports_nested_containers,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Container,
                                                        true,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            )
                                            .child(
                                                crate::buttons::button(
                                                    "config-generator-add-child-comment",
                                                )
                                                .label("子注释")
                                                .small()
                                                .disabled(
                                                    busy || !selected_local_draft_container,
                                                )
                                                .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, window, cx| {
                                                    page.add_visual_node(
                                                        VisualNodeKind::Comment,
                                                        true,
                                                        window,
                                                        cx,
                                                    )
                                                })),
                                            ),
                                    ),
                            )
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .debug_selector(|| {
                                        "config-generator-full-preview".to_owned()
                                    })
                                    .flex_1()
                                    .min_h(px(VISUAL_WORKSPACE_LAYOUT.preview_min_height))
                                    .rounded_lg()
                                    .border_1()
                                    .border_color(tokens.border)
                                    .bg(tokens.surface)
                                    .p_3()
                                    .flex()
                                    .flex_col()
                                    .gap_2()
                                    .child(section_label("完整配置预览", tokens))
                                    .child(visual_config_preview(
                                        &self.model.visual_document,
                                        contains_sensitive_values,
                                        tokens,
                                    )),
                            ),
                    ),
            )
            .into_any_element()
    }
}

impl Render for ConfigGeneratorPage {
    fn render(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> impl IntoElement {
        if self.visual_editor_reset_pending {
            self.visual_editor_reset_pending = false;
            self.reset_visual_editor(window, cx);
        }
        let tokens = self.shared.read(cx).theme.tokens();
        let error = self.error.clone();
        let notice = self.notice.clone();
        let mode = self.model.mode;
        let workspace = match mode {
            ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick => self.render_quick_workspace(tokens, cx),
            ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual => self.render_visual_workspace(tokens, cx),
        };

        let page_content = div()
            .id("config-generator-page-scroll")
            .size_full()
            .overflow_y_scroll()
            .track_scroll(&self.page_scroll)
            .flex()
            .flex_col()
            .gap_4()
            .child(
                div()
                    .flex_none()
                    .flex()
                    .items_end()
                    .gap_4()
                    .child(
                        div()
                            .flex_1()
                            .child(div().text_2xl().mb_2().child("配置生成"))
                            .child(
                                div()
                                    .text_sm()
                                    .text_color(tokens.muted)
                                    .child("选择产品，以 Baseline 为起点叠加多项配置，实时预览并保存完整配置文件；部署前仍需目标侧语法与行为验证。"),
                            ),
                    )
                    .when(mode == ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual, |header| {
                        header.child(
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .gap_2()
                                .child(
                                    crate::buttons::button("config-generator-mode-quick")
                                        .label("返回配置生成")
                                        .small()
                                        .on_click(cx.listener(|page, _, _, cx| {
                                            page.select_mode(ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick, cx)
                                        })),
                                )
                                .child(
                                    crate::buttons::button("config-generator-mode-visual")
                                        .label(ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual.label())
                                        .small()
                                        .selected(true)
                                        .disabled(true),
                                ),
                        )
                    }),
            )
            .when_some(error, |page, message| {
                page.child(message_banner(message, true, tokens))
            })
            .when_some(notice, |page, message| {
                page.child(message_banner(message, false, tokens))
            })
            .child(workspace);

        div()
            .id("config-generator-page")
            .relative()
            .size_full()
            .min_h_0()
            .child(page_content)
            .vertical_scrollbar(&self.page_scroll)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn generate_from_values(
    kind: NginxSiteKind,
    server_name: &str,
    listen_port: &str,
    static_root: &str,
    upstream: &str,
    security_headers: bool,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedNginxConfig> {
    let mut features = BTreeSet::new();
    if kind == NginxSiteKind::Static {
        features.extend([
            NginxSiteFeature::IndexFiles,
            NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles,
        ]);
    }
    if security_headers {
        features.extend([
            NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders,
            NginxSiteFeature::HideVersion,
        ]);
    }
    generate_from_values_with_features(
        kind,
        server_name,
        listen_port,
        static_root,
        upstream,
        NginxFeatureInputs {
            features: &features,
            forbidden_path: None,
            allowed_source: DEFAULT_ALLOWED_SOURCE,
        },
    )
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn generate_from_values_with_forbidden_path(
    kind: NginxSiteKind,
    server_name: &str,
    listen_port: &str,
    static_root: &str,
    upstream: &str,
    security_headers: bool,
    forbidden_path: Option<(&str, NginxForbiddenPathScope)>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedNginxConfig> {
    let mut features = BTreeSet::new();
    if kind == NginxSiteKind::Static {
        features.extend([
            NginxSiteFeature::IndexFiles,
            NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles,
        ]);
    }
    if security_headers {
        features.extend([
            NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders,
            NginxSiteFeature::HideVersion,
        ]);
    }
    if forbidden_path.is_some() {
        features.insert(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
    }
    generate_from_values_with_features(
        kind,
        server_name,
        listen_port,
        static_root,
        upstream,
        NginxFeatureInputs {
            features: &features,
            forbidden_path,
            allowed_source: DEFAULT_ALLOWED_SOURCE,
        },
    )
}

struct NginxFeatureInputs<'a> {
    features: &'a BTreeSet<NginxSiteFeature>,
    forbidden_path: Option<(&'a str, NginxForbiddenPathScope)>,
    allowed_source: &'a str,
}

fn generate_from_values_with_features(
    kind: NginxSiteKind,
    server_name: &str,
    listen_port: &str,
    static_root: &str,
    upstream: &str,
    feature_inputs: NginxFeatureInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedNginxConfig> {
    let NginxFeatureInputs {
        features,
        forbidden_path,
        allowed_source,
    } = feature_inputs;
    let names = NginxServerNames::parse(server_name)?;
    let port = NginxListenPort::parse(listen_port)?;
    let target = match kind {
        NginxSiteKind::Static => NginxSiteTarget::Static(NginxStaticRoot::parse(static_root)?),
        NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy => {
            NginxSiteTarget::ReverseProxy(NginxUpstream::parse(upstream)?)
        }
    };
    let mut config = NginxSiteConfig::new(names, port, target, false)?;
    for feature in NginxSiteFeature::ALL {
        config = config.with_feature(feature, features.contains(&feature))?;
    }
    if let Some((path, scope)) = forbidden_path {
        config = config.with_forbidden_path_rule(NginxForbiddenPathRule::new(
            NginxForbiddenPath::parse(path)?,
            scope,
        )?)?;
    }
    if features.contains(&NginxSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp) {
        config = config.with_allowed_source_rule(NginxAllowedSource::parse(allowed_source)?)?;
    }
    config.generate()
}

struct ApacheFeatureInputs<'a> {
    features: &'a BTreeSet<ApacheSiteFeature>,
    forbidden_path: Option<(&'a str, ApacheForbiddenPathScope)>,
    allowed_source: &'a str,
}

fn generate_apache_from_values_with_features(
    kind: ApacheSiteKind,
    server_name: &str,
    listen_port: &str,
    static_root: &str,
    upstream: &str,
    feature_inputs: ApacheFeatureInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedApacheConfig> {
    let ApacheFeatureInputs {
        features,
        forbidden_path,
        allowed_source,
    } = feature_inputs;
    let name = ApacheServerName::parse(server_name)?;
    let port = ApacheListenPort::parse(listen_port)?;
    let target = match kind {
        ApacheSiteKind::Static => ApacheSiteTarget::Static(ApacheStaticRoot::parse(static_root)?),
        ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy => {
            ApacheSiteTarget::ReverseProxy(ApacheUpstream::parse(upstream)?)
        }
    };
    let mut config = ApacheSiteConfig::new(name, port, target, false)?;
    for feature in ApacheSiteFeature::ALL {
        config = config.with_feature(feature, features.contains(&feature))?;
    }
    if let Some((path, scope)) = forbidden_path {
        config = config.with_forbidden_path_rule(ApacheForbiddenPathRule::new(
            ApacheForbiddenPath::parse(path)?,
            scope,
        )?)?;
    }
    if features.contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp) {
        config = config.with_allowed_source_rule(ApacheAllowedSource::parse(allowed_source)?)?;
    }
    config.generate()
}

struct MySqlFeatureValueInputs<'a> {
    remote_listener: &'a str,
    max_connections: &'a str,
    connect_timeout: &'a str,
    max_connect_errors: &'a str,
    wait_timeout: &'a str,
    interactive_timeout: &'a str,
    long_query_time: &'a str,
    log_error_verbosity: &'a str,
    server_id: &'a str,
    binlog_retention: &'a str,
}

fn parse_mysql_value<T>(field: &'static str, value: &str) -> CoreResult<T>
where
    T: std::str::FromStr,
    T::Err: fmt::Display,
{
    value.trim().parse().map_err(|error| CoreError::Validation {
        subject: "MySQL configuration value",
        message: format!("{field}: {error}"),
    })
}

fn generate_mysql_from_values_with_features(
    features: &BTreeSet<MySqlConfigFeature>,
    inputs: MySqlFeatureValueInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedMySqlConfig> {
    let version = MySqlConfigVersion::new(8, 4, 0).map_err(mysql_config_error)?;
    let mut config = MySqlConfig::baseline(version);
    for feature in MySqlConfigFeature::ALL {
        if !features.contains(&feature) {
            continue;
        }
        config = match feature {
            MySqlConfigFeature::RemoteListener => config
                .with_remote_listener(parse_mysql_value("bind_address", inputs.remote_listener)?),
            MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections => config.with_max_connections(parse_mysql_value(
                "max_connections",
                inputs.max_connections,
            )?),
            MySqlConfigFeature::ConnectTimeout => config.with_connect_timeout_seconds(
                parse_mysql_value("connect_timeout", inputs.connect_timeout)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnectErrors => config.with_max_connect_errors(
                parse_mysql_value("max_connect_errors", inputs.max_connect_errors)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::SessionTimeouts => config.with_session_timeouts_seconds(
                parse_mysql_value("wait_timeout", inputs.wait_timeout)?,
                parse_mysql_value("interactive_timeout", inputs.interactive_timeout)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog => config.with_slow_query_log_seconds(
                parse_mysql_value("long_query_time", inputs.long_query_time)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::LogErrorVerbosity => config.with_log_error_verbosity(
                parse_mysql_value("log_error_verbosity", inputs.log_error_verbosity)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging => {
                config.with_binary_logging(parse_mysql_value("server_id", inputs.server_id)?)
            }
            MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention => config.with_binary_log_retention_seconds(
                parse_mysql_value("binlog_expire_logs_seconds", inputs.binlog_retention)?,
            ),
            MySqlConfigFeature::DisableTcp
            | MySqlConfigFeature::SkipNameResolve
            | MySqlConfigFeature::GeneralLog
            | MySqlConfigFeature::DisableFileImportExport
            | MySqlConfigFeature::ReadOnlyReplica
            | MySqlConfigFeature::EnableLocalInfile => config.with_feature(feature, true),
        }
        .map_err(mysql_config_error)?;
    }
    config.generate().map_err(mysql_config_error)
}

struct PostgreSqlFeatureValueInputs<'a> {
    port: &'a str,
    max_connections: &'a str,
    superuser_reserved_connections: &'a str,
    reserved_connections: &'a str,
    authentication_timeout: &'a str,
    idle_in_transaction_timeout: &'a str,
    idle_session_timeout: &'a str,
    temporary_file_limit: &'a str,
    lock_wait_threshold: &'a str,
    autovacuum_log_threshold: &'a str,
    temporary_file_log_threshold: &'a str,
    wal_compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression,
}

fn generate_postgresql_from_values_with_features(
    version: PostgreSqlVersion,
    features: &BTreeSet<PostgreSqlConfigFeature>,
    inputs: PostgreSqlFeatureValueInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedPostgreSqlConfig> {
    let mut config =
        PostgreSqlConfig::baseline(version).with_port(PostgreSqlPort::parse(inputs.port)?);
    for feature in PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ALL {
        if !features.contains(&feature) {
            continue;
        }
        config = match feature {
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity => {
                config.with_connection_capacity(PostgreSqlConnectionCapacity::parse(
                    inputs.max_connections,
                    inputs.superuser_reserved_connections,
                    inputs.reserved_connections,
                )?)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AuthenticationTimeout => config.with_authentication_timeout(
                PostgreSqlAuthenticationTimeoutSeconds::parse(inputs.authentication_timeout)?,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleInTransactionGuard => config
                .with_idle_in_transaction_timeout(
                    PostgreSqlIdleInTransactionTimeoutSeconds::parse(
                        inputs.idle_in_transaction_timeout,
                    )?,
                ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IdleSessionGuard => config.with_idle_session_timeout(
                PostgreSqlIdleSessionTimeoutSeconds::parse(inputs.idle_session_timeout)?,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLimit => config.with_temporary_file_limit(
                PostgreSqlTemporaryFileLimitMebibytes::parse(inputs.temporary_file_limit)?,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::LockWaitLogging => config.with_lock_wait_logging_threshold(
                PostgreSqlLockWaitThresholdMilliseconds::parse(inputs.lock_wait_threshold)?,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AutovacuumLogging => config.with_autovacuum_logging_threshold(
                PostgreSqlAutovacuumLogThresholdMilliseconds::parse(
                    inputs.autovacuum_log_threshold,
                )?,
            ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::TemporaryFileLogging => config
                .with_temporary_file_logging_threshold(
                    PostgreSqlTemporaryFileLogThresholdKilobytes::parse(
                        inputs.temporary_file_log_threshold,
                    )?,
                ),
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::WalCompression => {
                config.with_wal_compression(inputs.wal_compression)
            }
            PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionLifecycleLogging
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::CheckpointLogging
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IoTiming
            | PostgreSqlConfigFeature::QueryIdentifier => config.with_feature(feature, true),
        }?;
    }
    config.generate()
}

struct MariaDbFeatureValueInputs<'a> {
    port: &'a str,
    max_connections: &'a str,
    connect_timeout: &'a str,
    wait_timeout: &'a str,
    interactive_timeout: &'a str,
    max_connect_errors: &'a str,
    max_allowed_packet: &'a str,
    slow_query_time: &'a str,
    server_id: &'a str,
    binlog_retention: &'a str,
    innodb_lock_wait_timeout: &'a str,
}

fn generate_mariadb_from_values_with_features(
    version: MariaDbVersion,
    features: &BTreeSet<MariaDbConfigFeature>,
    character_profile: MariaDbCharacterProfile,
    inputs: MariaDbFeatureValueInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedMariaDbConfig> {
    let mut config = MariaDbConfig::baseline(version).with_port(MariaDbPort::parse(inputs.port)?);
    for feature in MariaDbConfigFeature::ALL {
        if !features.contains(&feature) {
            continue;
        }
        config = match feature {
            MariaDbConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity => {
                config.with_max_connections(MariaDbMaxConnections::parse(inputs.max_connections)?)
            }
            MariaDbConfigFeature::HandshakeTimeout => config
                .with_connect_timeout(MariaDbConnectTimeoutSeconds::parse(inputs.connect_timeout)?),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SessionIdleTimeouts => config.with_session_idle_timeouts(
                MariaDbSessionIdleTimeouts::parse(inputs.wait_timeout, inputs.interactive_timeout)?,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::HostErrorThreshold => config.with_max_connect_errors(
                MariaDbMaxConnectErrors::parse(inputs.max_connect_errors)?,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::MaximumPacketSize => config.with_max_allowed_packet(
                MariaDbMaxAllowedPacketMebibytes::parse(inputs.max_allowed_packet)?,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog => config
                .with_slow_query_time(MariaDbSlowQueryTimeSeconds::parse(inputs.slow_query_time)?),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging => {
                config.with_server_id(MariaDbServerId::parse(inputs.server_id)?)
            }
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention => config.with_binary_log_retention(
                MariaDbBinaryLogRetentionSeconds::parse(inputs.binlog_retention)?,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation => {
                config.with_character_profile(character_profile)
            }
            MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbLockWaitTimeout => config.with_innodb_lock_wait_timeout(
                MariaDbInnoDbLockWaitTimeoutSeconds::parse(inputs.innodb_lock_wait_timeout)?,
            ),
            MariaDbConfigFeature::SkipNameResolve
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::ErrorLogFile
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogDurability
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbDurability
            | MariaDbConfigFeature::InnoDbDeadlockLogging => config.with_feature(feature, true),
        }?;
    }
    config.generate()
}

struct RedisFeatureValueInputs<'a> {
    data_directory: &'a str,
    port: &'a str,
    max_clients: &'a str,
    idle_timeout: &'a str,
    tcp_keepalive: &'a str,
    maxmemory_mib: &'a str,
    slowlog_threshold: &'a str,
    slowlog_length: &'a str,
    latency_threshold: &'a str,
}

fn parse_redis_number<T>(field: &'static str, value: &str) -> CoreResult<T>
where
    T: std::str::FromStr,
    T::Err: fmt::Display,
{
    value.trim().parse().map_err(|error| CoreError::Validation {
        subject: "Redis configuration value",
        message: format!("{field}: {error}"),
    })
}

fn generate_redis_from_values_with_features(
    version: RedisVersion,
    bind_profile: RedisBindProfile,
    features: &BTreeSet<RedisConfigFeature>,
    eviction_policy: RedisEvictionPolicy,
    append_fsync: RedisAppendFsync,
    inputs: RedisFeatureValueInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedRedisConfig> {
    let mut config = RedisConfig::baseline(version)
        .with_bind_profile(bind_profile)
        .with_port(RedisPort::parse(inputs.port)?)
        .with_data_directory(RedisDataDirectory::parse(inputs.data_directory)?)?;
    for feature in RedisConfigFeature::ALL {
        config = if features.contains(&feature) {
            match feature {
                RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients => {
                    config.with_max_clients(RedisMaxClients::parse(inputs.max_clients)?)
                }
                RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout => {
                    config.with_idle_timeout(RedisIdleTimeoutSeconds::parse(inputs.idle_timeout)?)
                }
                RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive => config
                    .with_tcp_keepalive(RedisTcpKeepaliveSeconds::parse(inputs.tcp_keepalive)?),
                RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit => {
                    config.with_memory_limit(RedisMaxmemory::parse_mebibytes(inputs.maxmemory_mib)?)
                }
                RedisConfigFeature::EvictionPolicy => config.with_eviction_policy(eviction_policy),
                RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence => config.with_append_fsync(append_fsync),
                RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence => config.with_feature(feature, true),
                RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog => config.with_slow_log(RedisSlowLogConfig::new(
                    parse_redis_number("slowlog-log-slower-than", inputs.slowlog_threshold)?,
                    parse_redis_number("slowlog-max-len", inputs.slowlog_length)?,
                )?),
                RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor => config.with_latency_threshold(
                    RedisLatencyThresholdMilliseconds::parse(inputs.latency_threshold)?,
                ),
            }
        } else {
            config.with_feature(feature, false)
        }?;
    }
    config.generate()
}

struct TomcatFeatureValueInputs<'a> {
    application_name: &'a str,
    port: &'a str,
    connection_timeout: &'a str,
    max_threads: &'a str,
    accept_count: &'a str,
    max_connections: &'a str,
    max_post_size: &'a str,
    max_swallow_size: &'a str,
    compression_min_size: &'a str,
}

fn generate_tomcat_from_values_with_features(
    version: TomcatVersionFamily,
    address: TomcatConnectorAddress,
    features: &BTreeSet<TomcatConfigFeature>,
    inputs: TomcatFeatureValueInputs<'_>,
) -> CoreResult<GeneratedTomcatQuickConfig> {
    let connector = TomcatConnectorConfig::new(
        address,
        TomcatConnectorPort::parse(inputs.port)?,
        TomcatConnectionTimeoutMillis::parse(inputs.connection_timeout)?,
        TomcatMaxThreads::parse(inputs.max_threads)?,
        TomcatAcceptCount::parse(inputs.accept_count)?,
        TomcatMaxConnections::parse(inputs.max_connections)?,
        TomcatFormParameterBytes::parse(inputs.max_post_size)?,
        TomcatAbortedUploadSwallowBytes::parse(inputs.max_swallow_size)?,
    )?;
    let mut config = TomcatConfig::baseline(version)
        .with_application_name(TomcatApplicationName::parse(inputs.application_name)?)
        .with_connector(connector)?;
    for feature in TomcatConfigFeature::ALL {
        config = config.with_feature(feature, features.contains(&feature))?;
    }
    if features.contains(&TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression) {
        config = config.with_compression_min_size(TomcatCompressionMinBytes::parse(
            inputs.compression_min_size,
        )?);
    }
    config.generate().map(GeneratedTomcatQuickConfig::new)
}

fn input_field(
    label: &'static str,
    help: &'static str,
    input: &Entity<InputState>,
    tokens: ThemeTokens,
) -> AnyElement {
    div()
        .child(section_label(label, tokens))
        .child(div().mt_2().child(Input::new(input).small()))
        .child(div().mt_1().text_xs().text_color(tokens.muted).child(help))
        .into_any_element()
}

fn section_label(label: &'static str, tokens: ThemeTokens) -> AnyElement {
    div()
        .text_sm()
        .font_weight(gpui::FontWeight::SEMIBOLD)
        .text_color(tokens.text)
        .child(label)
        .into_any_element()
}

fn message_banner(message: SharedString, is_error: bool, tokens: ThemeTokens) -> AnyElement {
    let color = if is_error {
        tokens.danger
    } else {
        tokens.success
    };
    div()
        .flex_none()
        .rounded_md()
        .border_1()
        .border_color(color)
        .p_3()
        .text_sm()
        .text_color(color)
        .child(message)
        .into_any_element()
}

const MINIMUM_PREVIEW_TEXT_CONTRAST: f32 = 4.5;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
struct ConfigPreviewColors {
    background: gpui::Rgba,
    code_text: gpui::Rgba,
}

impl ConfigPreviewColors {
    fn from_tokens(tokens: ThemeTokens) -> Self {
        let code_text =
            higher_contrast_color(tokens.text, tokens.surface, tokens.terminal_background);
        debug_assert!(
            contrast_ratio(code_text, tokens.terminal_background) >= MINIMUM_PREVIEW_TEXT_CONTRAST
        );
        Self {
            background: tokens.terminal_background,
            code_text,
        }
    }
}

fn higher_contrast_color(
    first: gpui::Rgba,
    second: gpui::Rgba,
    background: gpui::Rgba,
) -> gpui::Rgba {
    if contrast_ratio(first, background) >= contrast_ratio(second, background) {
        first
    } else {
        second
    }
}

fn contrast_ratio(first: gpui::Rgba, second: gpui::Rgba) -> f32 {
    let first = relative_luminance(first);
    let second = relative_luminance(second);
    (first.max(second) + 0.05) / (first.min(second) + 0.05)
}

fn relative_luminance(color: gpui::Rgba) -> f32 {
    fn linearize(channel: f32) -> f32 {
        if channel <= 0.04045 {
            channel / 12.92
        } else {
            ((channel + 0.055) / 1.055).powf(2.4)
        }
    }

    0.2126 * linearize(color.r) + 0.7152 * linearize(color.g) + 0.0722 * linearize(color.b)
}

fn config_preview(
    config: Option<&GeneratedQuickConfig>,
    tokens: ThemeTokens,
    scroll: &ScrollHandle,
) -> AnyElement {
    let colors = ConfigPreviewColors::from_tokens(tokens);
    let Some(config) = config else {
        return div()
            .flex_1()
            .min_h_0()
            .rounded_md()
            .bg(colors.background)
            .flex()
            .items_center()
            .justify_center()
            .text_sm()
            .text_color(colors.code_text)
            .child("修正左侧参数后会自动生成预览")
            .into_any_element();
    };
    let (lines, truncated) = bounded_preview_lines(config.contents());
    let line_count = preview_total_line_count(config.contents());
    div()
        .flex_1()
        .min_h_0()
        .rounded_md()
        .bg(colors.background)
        .flex()
        .flex_col()
        .font_family("Cascadia Mono")
        .text_xs()
        .text_color(colors.code_text)
        .child(
            div()
                .flex_1()
                .min_h_0()
                .relative()
                .child(
                    div()
                        .id("config-generator-quick-preview-scroll")
                        .debug_selector(|| "config-generator-quick-preview-scroll".to_owned())
                        .size_full()
                        .overflow_scroll()
                        .track_scroll(scroll)
                        .p_3()
                        .children(lines.into_iter().enumerate().map(|(index, line)| {
                            div()
                                .flex()
                                .whitespace_nowrap()
                                .child(
                                    div()
                                        .w(px(34.0))
                                        .flex_none()
                                        .pr_3()
                                        .text_right()
                                        .child((index + 1).to_string()),
                                )
                                .child(if line.is_empty() {
                                    " ".to_owned()
                                } else {
                                    line
                                })
                        }))
                        .when(truncated, |preview| {
                            preview.child(
                                div()
                                    .mt_2()
                                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                                    .child("预览已截断；复制和保存仍使用完整配置。"),
                            )
                        }),
                )
                .vertical_scrollbar(scroll),
        )
        .child(
            div()
                .flex_none()
                .border_t_1()
                .border_color(tokens.border)
                .px_3()
                .py_2()
                .flex()
                .justify_between()
                .gap_3()
                .child(format!("语言：{}", config.language_label()))
                .child(format!("共 {line_count} 行 · 自动换行：关")),
        )
        .into_any_element()
}

fn visual_config_preview(
    document: &VisualDocument,
    contains_sensitive_values: bool,
    tokens: ThemeTokens,
) -> AnyElement {
    let colors = ConfigPreviewColors::from_tokens(tokens);
    if contains_sensitive_values {
        return div()
            .flex_1()
            .min_h_0()
            .rounded_md()
            .bg(colors.background)
            .flex()
            .items_center()
            .justify_center()
            .p_4()
            .text_sm()
            .text_color(tokens.warning)
            .child("检测到敏感内容，完整预览已隐藏；另存本地草稿会保留原始字节，不能应用/部署。")
            .into_any_element();
    }
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(document.raw_bytes());
    let (lines, truncated) = bounded_preview_lines(text.as_ref());
    div()
        .flex_1()
        .min_h_0()
        .overflow_scrollbar()
        .rounded_md()
        .bg(colors.background)
        .p_3()
        .font_family("Cascadia Mono")
        .text_xs()
        .text_color(colors.code_text)
        .children(lines.into_iter().map(|line| {
            div().child(if line.is_empty() {
                " ".to_owned()
            } else {
                line
            })
        }))
        .when(truncated, |preview| {
            preview.child(
                div()
                    .mt_2()
                    .text_color(tokens.warning)
                    .child("预览已截断；复制和保存仍使用完整配置。"),
            )
        })
        .into_any_element()
}

fn bounded_preview_lines(text: &str) -> (Vec<String>, bool) {
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    let mut chars = 0usize;
    let mut truncated = false;
    for line in text.lines() {
        let remaining = MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS.saturating_sub(chars);
        if lines.len() == MAX_PREVIEW_LINES || remaining == 0 {
            truncated = true;
            break;
        }
        let mut characters = line.chars();
        let bounded = characters.by_ref().take(remaining).collect::<String>();
        chars += bounded.chars().count();
        if characters.next().is_some() {
            truncated = true;
        }
        lines.push(bounded);
        if truncated {
            break;
        }
    }
    if lines.is_empty() {
        lines.push(String::new());
    }
    (lines, truncated)
}

fn preview_total_line_count(text: &str) -> usize {
    text.lines().count().max(1)
}

    #[test]
    fn database_bundle_parameter_labels_disambiguate_every_mariadb_container() {
        let schemas = DATABASE_CONFIG_SCHEMAS
            .iter()
            .filter(|schema| schema.binding.product == DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert_eq!(schemas.len(), 2);

        for schema in schemas {
            let labels = schema
                .parameters
                .iter()
                .filter(|parameter| parameter.canonical_key == "wait_timeout")
                .map(database_goal_parameter_label)
                .collect::<HashSet<_>>();
            assert_eq!(labels.len(), 9, "{}", schema.version_label);
            assert!(labels.iter().all(|label| label.ends_with("· wait_timeout")));
            assert!(!labels.contains("根级配置 · wait_timeout"));
        }
    }

    fn database_artifact(key: &str) -> &'static DatabaseConfigArtifactDefinition {
        lovelyres_core::database_config::DATABASE_CONFIG_ARTIFACTS
            .iter()
            .find(|artifact| artifact.key == key)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing database configuration artifact {key}"))
    }

    fn model_with_visual_document(document: VisualDocument) -> ConfigGeneratorModel {
        let generated = generate_from_values(
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            DEFAULT_SERVER_NAME,
            DEFAULT_LISTEN_PORT,
            DEFAULT_STATIC_ROOT,
            DEFAULT_UPSTREAM,
            true,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut model = ConfigGeneratorModel::new(generated);
        model.replace_visual_document(document);
        model
    }

    fn visual_selector_contains(artifact: &DatabaseConfigArtifactDefinition) -> bool {
        artifacts_for(artifact.product)
            .filter(|candidate| database_visual_parser(candidate).is_some())
            .any(|candidate| candidate.key == artifact.key)
    }

    fn assert_local_draft_mutation_denied<T>(result: CoreResult<T>, key: &str) {
        let error = match result {
            Ok(_) => panic!("read-only artifact {key} accepted a local draft mutation"),
            Err(error) => error,
        };
        assert!(
            error.to_string().contains("local draft mutation"),
            "unexpected {key} mutation error: {error}"
        );
    }

    fn filtered_row_names<'a>(rows: &'a [VisualRow], indices: &[usize]) -> Vec<&'a str> {
        indices
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|index| rows.get(*index).and_then(VisualRow::name))
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn goal_catalog_product_search_and_subject_inference_are_bounded_and_exact() {
        assert!(catalog_product_matches("nginx", "Nginx"));
        assert!(catalog_product_matches("达梦", "DM / 达梦"));
        assert!(!catalog_product_matches("Kafka", "Nginx"));
        assert_eq!(
            unique_goal_subject_for_query("Nginx 反向代理"),
            Some(ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(
                MiddlewareProduct::Nginx
            ))
        );
        assert_eq!(
            unique_goal_subject_for_query("PostgreSQL max_connections"),
            Some(ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(
                DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql
            ))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn nginx_configuration_catalog_has_a_useful_baseline_and_searchable_rows() {
        assert!(
            NginxSiteFeature::ALL.len() >= 31,
            "the Nginx catalog must keep the expanded site and proxy recipes"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            default_nginx_features(NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy),
            BTreeSet::from([
                NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders,
                NginxSiteFeature::DisableDirectoryListing,
                NginxSiteFeature::HideVersion,
            ])
        );
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "反向代理"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403),
            "403"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403),
            "/admin 外部访问全部返回 403"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::GzipCompression),
            "压缩"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::HealthCheck),
            "存活探测"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ProxyTimeouts),
            "上游超时"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback),
            "前端路由"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ClientIoTimeouts),
            "客户端超时"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::HttpsUpstreamSni),
            "HTTPS 上游"
        ));
        assert!(!nginx_feature_applicable(
            NginxSiteFeature::HttpsUpstreamSni,
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            "http://upstream.example"
        ));
        assert!(!nginx_feature_applicable(
            NginxSiteFeature::HttpsUpstreamSni,
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            "https://127.0.0.1:8443"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_feature_applicable(
            NginxSiteFeature::HttpsUpstreamSni,
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            "https://upstream.example:8443"
        ));
        assert!(!nginx_feature_applicable(
            NginxSiteFeature::LimitRangeRequests,
            NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy,
            "http://upstream.example"
        ));
        assert!(nginx_feature_applicable(
            NginxSiteFeature::LimitRangeRequests,
            NginxSiteKind::Static,
            "http://upstream.example"
        ));
        assert!(!nginx_configuration_matches(
            NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders),
            "websocket"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn apache_configuration_catalog_has_a_useful_baseline_and_searchable_rows() {
        assert_eq!(
            ApacheSiteFeature::ALL.len(),
            22,
            "the first Apache catalog is a reviewed closed set"
        );
        let defaults = default_apache_features(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
        assert!(!defaults.is_empty());
        assert!(defaults.iter().all(|feature| {
            feature.default_enabled() && feature.supports_kind(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy)
        }));
        assert_eq!(
            ApacheSiteKind::Static.baseline_required_modules(),
            &["authz_core_module"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy.baseline_required_modules(),
            &["proxy_module", "proxy_http_module"]
        );
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "VirtualHost"
        ));
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403),
            "403"
        ));
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403),
            "/admin 外部访问全部返回 403"
        ));
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::GzipCompression),
            "压缩"
        ));
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ProxyTimeouts),
            "代理超时"
        ));
        assert!(apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::SpaFallback),
            "SPA"
        ));
        assert!(!ApacheSiteFeature::ProxyTimeouts.supports_kind(ApacheSiteKind::Static));
        assert!(!ApacheSiteFeature::SpaFallback.supports_kind(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy));
        assert!(!apache_configuration_matches(
            ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders),
            "websocket"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn postgresql_and_mariadb_quick_catalogs_are_closed_searchable_and_typed() {
        assert_eq!(
            PostgreSqlVersion::ALL.map(PostgreSqlVersion::label),
            ["14", "15", "16", "17", "18"]
        );
        assert_eq!(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ALL.len(), 13);
        assert!(default_postgresql_features().is_empty());
        assert!(postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "PostgreSQL 14"
        ));
        assert!(postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity),
            "reserved_connections"
        ));
        assert!(postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::AutovacuumLogging),
            "自动清理日志"
        ));
        assert!(postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::IoTiming),
            "track_wal_io_timing"
        ));
        assert!(postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::QueryIdentifier),
            "pg_stat_statements"
        ));
        assert!(!postgresql_configuration_matches(
            PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::LockWaitLogging),
            "binlog"
        ));

        let postgresql_features = BTreeSet::from(PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ALL);
        let postgresql = generate_postgresql_from_values_with_features(
            PostgreSqlVersion::V18,
            &postgresql_features,
            PostgreSqlFeatureValueInputs {
                port: "5544",
                max_connections: "240",
                superuser_reserved_connections: "5",
                reserved_connections: "2",
                authentication_timeout: "45",
                idle_in_transaction_timeout: "600",
                idle_session_timeout: "1800",
                temporary_file_limit: "2048",
                lock_wait_threshold: "1500",
                autovacuum_log_threshold: "2000",
                temporary_file_log_threshold: "8192",
                wal_compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression::Pglz,
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        for expected in [
            "PostgreSQL 18 new empty-cluster",
            "port = 5544",
            "max_connections = 240",
            "reserved_connections = 2",
            "superuser_reserved_connections = 5",
            "authentication_timeout = 45s",
            "idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = 600s",
            "idle_session_timeout = 1800s",
            "temp_file_limit = 2048MB",
            "deadlock_timeout = 1500ms",
            "log_lock_waits = on",
            "log_checkpoints = on",
            "log_autovacuum_min_duration = 2000ms",
            "log_temp_files = 8192kB",
            "track_io_timing = on",
            "track_wal_io_timing = on",
            "wal_compression = pglz",
            "compute_query_id = on",
        ] {
            assert!(
                postgresql.contents().contains(expected),
                "missing {expected}"
            );
        }
        assert_eq!(
            postgresql.suggested_file_name(),
            "lovelyres-postgresql-18-new-empty-cluster.conf"
        );
        assert!(
            generate_postgresql_from_values_with_features(
                PostgreSqlVersion::V15,
                &BTreeSet::from([PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity]),
                PostgreSqlFeatureValueInputs {
                    port: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_PORT,
                    max_connections: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                    superuser_reserved_connections:
                        DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_SUPERUSER_RESERVED_CONNECTIONS,
                    reserved_connections: "1",
                    authentication_timeout: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_AUTHENTICATION_TIMEOUT,
                    idle_in_transaction_timeout: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_IDLE_IN_TRANSACTION_TIMEOUT,
                    idle_session_timeout: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_IDLE_SESSION_TIMEOUT,
                    temporary_file_limit: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_TEMPORARY_FILE_LIMIT_MIB,
                    lock_wait_threshold: DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_LOCK_WAIT_THRESHOLD_MILLISECONDS,
                    autovacuum_log_threshold:
                        DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_AUTOVACUUM_LOG_THRESHOLD_MILLISECONDS,
                    temporary_file_log_threshold:
                        DEFAULT_POSTGRESQL_TEMPORARY_FILE_LOG_THRESHOLD_KILOBYTES,
                    wal_compression: PostgreSqlWalCompression::Pglz,
                },
            )
            .is_err(),
            "PostgreSQL 15 must fail closed when a 16+ reserved slot is supplied"
        );

        assert_eq!(
            MariaDbVersion::ALL.map(MariaDbVersion::label),
            ["10.11", "11.4", "11.8", "12.3"]
        );
        assert_eq!(MariaDbConfigFeature::ALL.len(), 15);
        assert!(default_mariadb_features().is_empty());
        assert!(mariadb_configuration_matches(
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "Community LTS 11.8"
        ));
        assert!(mariadb_configuration_matches(
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention),
            "自动清理"
        ));
        assert!(!mariadb_configuration_matches(
            MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog),
            "postgresql"
        ));

        let mariadb_features = BTreeSet::from([
            MariaDbConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity,
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging,
            MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention,
            MariaDbConfigFeature::CharacterSetAndCollation,
        ]);
        let mariadb = generate_mariadb_from_values_with_features(
            MariaDbVersion::V12_3,
            &mariadb_features,
            MariaDbCharacterProfile::UnicodeBinary,
            MariaDbFeatureValueInputs {
                port: "3310",
                max_connections: "350",
                connect_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                wait_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
                interactive_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_INTERACTIVE_TIMEOUT,
                max_connect_errors: DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_CONNECT_ERRORS,
                max_allowed_packet: DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_ALLOWED_PACKET_MIB,
                slow_query_time: DEFAULT_MARIADB_SLOW_QUERY_TIME,
                server_id: "42",
                binlog_retention: "86400",
                innodb_lock_wait_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_INNODB_LOCK_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        for expected in [
            "MariaDB 12.3 LTS new empty-instance",
            "port = 3310",
            "max_connections = 350",
            "log_bin = mariadb-bin",
            "server_id = 42",
            "binlog_expire_logs_seconds = 86400",
            "collation_server = utf8mb4_bin",
        ] {
            assert!(mariadb.contents().contains(expected), "missing {expected}");
        }
        assert_eq!(
            mariadb.suggested_file_name(),
            "lovelyres-mariadb-12.3-new-empty-instance.cnf"
        );
        assert!(
            generate_mariadb_from_values_with_features(
                MariaDbVersion::V11_4,
                &BTreeSet::from([MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention]),
                MariaDbCharacterProfile::UnicodeCaseInsensitive,
                MariaDbFeatureValueInputs {
                    port: DEFAULT_MARIADB_PORT,
                    max_connections: DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                    connect_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                    wait_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
                    interactive_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_INTERACTIVE_TIMEOUT,
                    max_connect_errors: DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_CONNECT_ERRORS,
                    max_allowed_packet: DEFAULT_MARIADB_MAX_ALLOWED_PACKET_MIB,
                    slow_query_time: DEFAULT_MARIADB_SLOW_QUERY_TIME,
                    server_id: DEFAULT_MARIADB_SERVER_ID,
                    binlog_retention: DEFAULT_MARIADB_BINLOG_RETENTION,
                    innodb_lock_wait_timeout: DEFAULT_MARIADB_INNODB_LOCK_WAIT_TIMEOUT,
                },
            )
            .is_err(),
            "MariaDB binlog retention must fail closed without BinaryLogging"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn redis_configuration_catalog_and_latest_empty_instance_baseline_are_searchable() {
        assert_eq!(RedisVersion::ALL.len(), 9);
        assert_eq!(RedisVersion::ALL.last(), Some(&RedisVersion::V8_10));
        assert_eq!(
            default_redis_features(),
            BTreeSet::from([
                RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients,
                RedisConfigFeature::TcpKeepalive,
                RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit,
                RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence,
                RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence,
                RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog,
            ])
        );
        assert!(redis_configuration_matches(
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "Redis 8.10 新建空实例"
        ));
        assert!(redis_configuration_matches(
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit),
            "maxmemory 内存上限"
        ));
        assert!(redis_configuration_matches(
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence),
            "AOF fsync"
        ));
        assert!(redis_configuration_matches(
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor),
            "延迟监控"
        ));
        assert!(!redis_configuration_matches(
            RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::SlowLog),
            "websocket"
        ));

        let generated = generate_redis_from_values_with_features(
            RedisVersion::V8_10,
            RedisBindProfile::DualStackLoopback,
            &default_redis_features(),
            RedisEvictionPolicy::NoEviction,
            RedisAppendFsync::EverySecond,
            RedisFeatureValueInputs {
                data_directory: DEFAULT_REDIS_DATA_DIRECTORY,
                port: DEFAULT_REDIS_PORT,
                max_clients: DEFAULT_REDIS_MAX_CLIENTS,
                idle_timeout: DEFAULT_REDIS_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
                tcp_keepalive: DEFAULT_REDIS_TCP_KEEPALIVE,
                maxmemory_mib: DEFAULT_REDIS_MAXMEMORY_MIB,
                slowlog_threshold: DEFAULT_REDIS_SLOWLOG_THRESHOLD,
                slowlog_length: DEFAULT_REDIS_SLOWLOG_LENGTH,
                latency_threshold: DEFAULT_REDIS_LATENCY_THRESHOLD,
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(generated.version(), RedisVersion::V8_10);
        assert!(generated.contents().contains("Redis Open Source 8.10"));
        assert!(generated.contents().contains("EMPTY INSTANCE ONLY"));
        assert!(
            generated
                .contents()
                .contains("appenddirname \"appendonlydir\"")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn tomcat_configuration_catalog_and_default_three_artifact_baseline_are_searchable() {
        assert_eq!(TomcatVersionFamily::ALL.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(TomcatConfigFeature::ALL.len(), 9);
        assert_eq!(
            default_tomcat_features(),
            BTreeSet::from([
                TomcatConfigFeature::ConnectorLimits,
                TomcatConfigFeature::HttpOnlySessionCookies,
                TomcatConfigFeature::HeaderSecurityFilter,
            ])
        );
        assert!(tomcat_configuration_matches(
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline,
            "Tomcat 11 Jakarta Servlet"
        ));
        assert!(tomcat_configuration_matches(
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::ConnectorLimits),
            "maxPostSize 参数转换"
        ));
        assert!(tomcat_configuration_matches(
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests),
            "全部返回 403"
        ));
        assert!(!tomcat_configuration_matches(
            TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression),
            "redis aof"
        ));
        assert!(
            TomcatConnectorField::MaxPostSize
                .summary()
                .contains("不是通用请求体或上传大小上限")
        );
        assert!(
            TomcatConnectorField::MaxSwallowSize
                .summary()
                .contains("不是正常上传大小上限")
        );

        let generated = generate_tomcat_from_values_with_features(
            TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0,
            TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv4Loopback,
            &default_tomcat_features(),
            TomcatFeatureValueInputs {
                application_name: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_APPLICATION_NAME,
                port: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_PORT,
                connection_timeout: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT,
                max_threads: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_THREADS,
                accept_count: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_ACCEPT_COUNT,
                max_connections: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_CONNECTIONS,
                max_post_size: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_POST_SIZE,
                max_swallow_size: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_MAX_SWALLOW_SIZE,
                compression_min_size: DEFAULT_TOMCAT_COMPRESSION_MIN_SIZE,
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(generated.bundle.version(), TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0);
        for path in [
            "conf/server.xml",
            "webapps/ROOT/META-INF/context.xml",
            "webapps/ROOT/WEB-INF/web.xml",
        ] {
            assert!(
                generated
                    .contents()
                    .contains(&format!("===== BEGIN {path} ====="))
            );
            assert!(
                generated
                    .contents()
                    .contains(&format!("===== END {path} ====="))
            );
        }
        assert!(generated.contents().contains("web-app_6_1.xsd"));
        assert!(generated.contents().contains("maxPostSize=\"2097152\""));
    }

    #[test]
    fn goal_catalog_middleware_groups_cover_the_registered_products_exactly_once() {
        let grouped = WEB_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS
            .into_iter()
            .chain(APPLICATION_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS)
            .chain(CACHE_MIDDLEWARE_PRODUCTS)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert_eq!(grouped.len(), MiddlewareProduct::ALL.len());
        assert_eq!(
            grouped.iter().copied().collect::<HashSet<_>>(),
            MiddlewareProduct::ALL.into_iter().collect::<HashSet<_>>()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn quick_configuration_source_does_not_restore_the_removed_legacy_filter_badges() {
        let source = include_str!("config_generator.rs");
        let content_badge = ["内容：", "已审配置"].concat();
        let product_badge = ["适用产品：", "Nginx"].concat();
        assert!(!source.contains(&content_badge));
        assert!(!source.contains(&product_badge));
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn goal_catalog_reference_viewport_keeps_three_columns_visible_and_non_overlapping(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-catalog-layout-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(800.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });

        let products = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-goal-product-catalog")
            .expect("product catalog bounds");
        let configurations = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-goal-configuration-list")
            .expect("configuration list bounds");
        let preview = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-goal-preview-panel")
            .expect("preview panel bounds");
        assert!(products.size.width >= px(230.0));
        assert!(products.origin.x + products.size.width <= configurations.origin.x);
        assert!(configurations.origin.x + configurations.size.width <= preview.origin.x);
        assert!(preview.origin.x + preview.size.width <= viewport.width);
        assert_eq!(products.origin.y, configurations.origin.y);
        assert_eq!(configurations.origin.y, preview.origin.y);
        assert!(products.size.height > px(500.0));
        assert!(configurations.size.height > px(500.0));
        assert!(preview.size.height > px(500.0));
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-table")
                .is_some(),
            "the middle column must render the flat configuration table"
        );
        let inline_editor = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-editor")
            .expect("selected configuration editor bounds");
        let baseline_row = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-row-baseline")
            .expect("baseline table row bounds");
        assert!(
            baseline_row.size.height <= px(56.0),
            "narrow table columns must truncate instead of expanding one row vertically"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            inline_editor.origin.y,
            baseline_row.origin.y + baseline_row.size.height,
            "the active editor must start directly below its configuration row"
        );
        let page_scroll = page.update(cx, |page, _| page.page_scroll.clone());
        assert_eq!(
            page_scroll.max_offset().height,
            px(0.0),
            "the reference viewport must keep scrolling inside the three panels"
        );

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_quick_configurations_stack_and_remove_independently(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                let baseline = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(baseline.contains("proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3000;"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("server_tokens off;"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("add_header X-Content-Type-Options"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("autoindex off;"));
                assert!(!baseline.contains("gzip on;"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("proxy_set_header Host $proxy_host;"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr;"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("proxy_set_header Forwarded \"\";"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("不是 Host 访问白名单"));

                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::StaticAssetCaching),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(stacked.contains("location /assets/"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("expires 7d;"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("limit_except GET HEAD"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3000;"));
                assert_eq!(stacked.matches("    location / {").count(), 1);

                page.remove_nginx_configuration(NginxSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods, cx);
                let remaining = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!remaining.contains("limit_except GET HEAD"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("location /assets/"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("expires 7d;"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3000;"));
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_remove_button_click_removes_feature_and_updates_preview_and_row_state(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-nginx-remove-click-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("add_header X-Content-Type-Options")
                );
                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });

        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(800.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let remove = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-remove-security-headers")
            .expect("rendered Nginx remove button bounds");
        cx.simulate_mouse_move(remove.center(), None, Modifiers::default());
        cx.simulate_click(remove.center(), Modifiers::default());
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });

        page.update(cx, |page, _| {
            assert!(
                !page
                    .model
                    .nginx_features
                    .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders)
            );
            assert_eq!(
                page.model.active_nginx_configuration,
                NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline
            );
            assert!(
                !page
                    .model
                    .generated
                    .as_ref()
                    .unwrap()
                    .contents()
                    .contains("add_header X-Content-Type-Options")
            );
        });
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-row-security-headers")
                .is_some(),
            "the now-unadded feature row must remain selectable"
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-select-security-headers")
                .is_some(),
            "the row action must render in its unadded state"
        );

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn apache_quick_configurations_stack_remove_reset_and_open_visual(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-apache-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let baseline = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                assert!(baseline.contains("<VirtualHost *:80>"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("ProxyRequests Off"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("ProxyPass"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("ProxyPassReverse"));

                page.open_apache_configuration(
                    ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_apache_configuration(
                    ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ClientBodyLimit),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(stacked.contains("Require method GET HEAD"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("LimitRequestBody 10485760"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("ProxyPass"));

                page.remove_apache_configuration(ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .apache_features
                        .contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace)
                );
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .apache_features
                        .contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods)
                );
                assert!(page.model.generated.is_some());

                page.open_apache_configuration(
                    ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .apache_features
                        .contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::DisableTrace)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .apache_features
                        .contains(&ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("Require method GET HEAD")
                );

                page.remove_apache_configuration(ApacheSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods, cx);
                let remaining = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!remaining.contains("Require method GET HEAD"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("LimitRequestBody 10485760"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("ProxyPass"));

                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.apache_kind, ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.apache_features,
                    default_apache_features(ApacheSiteKind::ReverseProxy)
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(), baseline);

                let quick = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                page.load_quick_into_visual(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.visual_document.kind(),
                    VisualDocumentKind::Apache
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.visual_document.raw_bytes(), quick.as_bytes());
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn mysql_quick_configurations_stack_remove_reset_preview_save_and_open_visual(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-mysql-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let baseline = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::MySql(_))
                ));
                assert!(baseline.contains("# Target version: MySQL 8.4.0"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("[mysqld]"));
