                assert!(baseline.contains("bind_address = 127.0.0.1"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("port = 3306"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("mysqlx = OFF"));
                assert!(baseline.contains("local_infile = OFF"));

                page.mysql_max_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("400", window, cx));
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(stacked.contains("max_connections = 400"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("slow_query_log = ON"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("long_query_time = 10"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("log_bin = binlog"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("server_id = 1"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("binlog_expire_logs_seconds = 2592000"));
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .mysql_features
                        .contains(&MySqlConfigFeature::BinaryLogging)
                );

                page.remove_mysql_configuration(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections, cx);
                let remaining = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!remaining.contains("max_connections = 400"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("slow_query_log = ON"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("binlog_expire_logs_seconds = 2592000"));

                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert!(page.model.mysql_features.is_empty());
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.active_mysql_configuration,
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Baseline
                );
                assert_eq!(page.mysql_max_connections_input.read(cx).value(), "151");
                assert_eq!(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(), baseline);

                let generated = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap();
                assert!(generated.matches_subject(page.model.selected_goal_subject));
                let destination = root.join("mysql-quick.cnf");
                generated.write_new(&destination).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&destination).unwrap(), baseline);

                page.load_quick_into_visual(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.visual_document.kind(),
                    VisualDocumentKind::Database(database_artifact("mysql-option-file"))
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.visual_document.raw_bytes(), baseline.as_bytes());
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn postgresql_and_mariadb_quick_interactions_are_isolated_and_save_create_new(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-postgresql-mariadb-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.postgresql_version, PostgreSqlVersion::V18);
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::PostgreSql(_))
                ));
                let postgresql_baseline =
                    page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                assert!(postgresql_baseline.contains("listen_addresses = 'localhost'"));
                assert!(postgresql_baseline.contains("port = 5432"));

                page.postgresql_max_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("240", window, cx));
                page.postgresql_reserved_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("2", window, cx));
                page.open_postgresql_configuration(
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_postgresql_configuration(
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionLifecycleLogging,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let postgresql_stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(postgresql_stacked.contains("max_connections = 240"));
                assert!(postgresql_stacked.contains("reserved_connections = 2"));
                assert!(
                    postgresql_stacked
                        .contains("log_connections = 'receipt,authentication,authorization'")
                );

                page.select_postgresql_version(PostgreSqlVersion::V15, window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.postgresql_reserved_connections_input.read(cx).value(),
                    "0"
                );
                let version_15 = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(version_15.contains("PostgreSQL 15 new empty-cluster"));
                assert!(!version_15.contains("\nreserved_connections ="));
                assert!(version_15.contains("log_connections = on"));

                page.remove_postgresql_configuration(
                    PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionCapacity,
                    cx,
                );
                let postgresql_remaining = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!postgresql_remaining.contains("max_connections = 240"));
                assert!(postgresql_remaining.contains("log_connections = on"));

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.mariadb_version, MariaDbVersion::V12_3);
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::MariaDb(_))
                ));
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .postgresql_features
                        .contains(&PostgreSqlConfigFeature::ConnectionLifecycleLogging),
                    "switching products must retain PostgreSQL draft state"
                );

                page.mariadb_server_id_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("42", window, cx));
                page.mariadb_binlog_retention_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("86400", window, cx));
                page.open_mariadb_configuration(
                    MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_mariadb_configuration(
                    MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogDurability,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .mariadb_features
                        .contains(&MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging)
                );
                let mariadb_stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(mariadb_stacked.contains("server_id = 42"));
                assert!(mariadb_stacked.contains("binlog_expire_logs_seconds = 86400"));
                assert!(mariadb_stacked.contains("sync_binlog = 1"));

                page.remove_mariadb_configuration(MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogging, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .mariadb_features
                        .contains(&MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention)
                );
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .mariadb_features
                        .contains(&MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogDurability)
                );
                let without_binlog = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!without_binlog.contains("log_bin = mariadb-bin"));
                assert!(!without_binlog.contains("sync_binlog = 1"));

                page.select_mariadb_version(MariaDbVersion::V10_11, cx);
                page.open_mariadb_configuration(
                    MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("MariaDB 10.11 LTS")
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.postgresql_version, PostgreSqlVersion::V18);
                assert!(page.model.postgresql_features.is_empty());
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(),
                    postgresql_baseline
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.mariadb_version, MariaDbVersion::V10_11);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .mariadb_features
                        .contains(&MariaDbConfigFeature::SlowQueryLog),
                    "resetting PostgreSQL must not reset MariaDB state"
                );

                let postgresql_payload = page.save_payload().unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    postgresql_payload.suggested_file_name(),
                    "lovelyres-postgresql-18-new-empty-cluster.conf"
                );
                let postgresql_destination = root.join("postgresql-quick.conf");
                postgresql_payload
                    .write_new(&postgresql_destination)
                    .unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    fs::read_to_string(&postgresql_destination).unwrap(),
                    postgresql_baseline
                );
                assert!(
                    postgresql_payload
                        .write_new(&postgresql_destination)
                        .is_err()
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let mariadb_before_reset =
                    page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                let mariadb_visual =
                    VisualDocument::from_quick(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap()).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    mariadb_visual.kind(),
                    VisualDocumentKind::Database(database_artifact("mariadb-option-file"))
                );
                assert_eq!(mariadb_visual.raw_bytes(), mariadb_before_reset.as_bytes());
                let mariadb_payload = page.save_payload().unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    mariadb_payload.suggested_file_name(),
                    "lovelyres-mariadb-10.11-new-empty-instance.cnf"
                );
                let mariadb_destination = root.join("mariadb-quick.cnf");
                mariadb_payload.write_new(&mariadb_destination).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    fs::read_to_string(&mariadb_destination).unwrap(),
                    mariadb_before_reset
                );
                assert!(mariadb_payload.write_new(&mariadb_destination).is_err());

                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mariadb_version, MariaDbVersion::V12_3);
                assert!(page.model.mariadb_features.is_empty());
                assert_eq!(
                    page.mariadb_server_id_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    DEFAULT_MARIADB_SERVER_ID
                );
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn redis_quick_configurations_stack_remove_reset_and_save_without_overwrite(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-redis-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let baseline = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                assert_eq!(page.model.redis_version, RedisVersion::V8_10);
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Redis(_))
                ));
                for expected in [
                    "Redis Open Source 8.10",
                    "EMPTY INSTANCE ONLY",
                    "bind 127.0.0.1 -::1",
                    "protected-mode yes",
                    "port 6379",
                    "dir /var/lib/redis",
                    "maxclients 10000",
                    "maxmemory 536870912",
                    "appendonly yes",
                    "appendfsync everysec",
                    "save 3600 1",
                    "slowlog-log-slower-than 10000",
                ] {
                    assert!(baseline.contains(expected), "missing {expected}");
                }

                page.redis_idle_timeout_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("600", window, cx));
                page.open_redis_configuration(
                    RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.redis_latency_threshold_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("50", window, cx));
                page.open_redis_configuration(
                    RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::LatencyMonitor),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.select_redis_eviction_policy(RedisEvictionPolicy::AllKeysLru, cx);
                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(stacked.contains("timeout 600"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("latency-monitor-threshold 50"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("maxmemory-policy allkeys-lru"));

                page.remove_redis_configuration(RedisConfigFeature::IdleClientTimeout, cx);
                let remaining = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(remaining.contains("timeout 0"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("latency-monitor-threshold 50"));
                assert!(remaining.contains("maxmemory-policy allkeys-lru"));

                page.remove_redis_configuration(RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit, cx);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .redis_features
                        .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::MemoryLimit)
                );
                assert!(page.model.generated.is_some());
                assert!(page.error.is_some());

                page.remove_redis_configuration(RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .redis_features
                        .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::AofPersistence)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .redis_features
                        .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence)
                );
                let rdb_only = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                assert!(rdb_only.contains("appendonly no"));
                assert!(rdb_only.contains("save 3600 1"));

                page.remove_redis_configuration(RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence, cx);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .redis_features
                        .contains(&RedisConfigFeature::RdbPersistence)
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(), rdb_only);

                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.redis_version, RedisVersion::V8_10);
                assert_eq!(page.model.redis_features, default_redis_features());
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.active_redis_configuration,
                    RedisConfigurationSelection::Baseline
                );
                assert_eq!(
                    page.redis_maxmemory_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_MAXMEMORY_MIB
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(), baseline);

                page.copy_current(cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    cx.read_from_clipboard()
                        .and_then(|item| item.text())
                        .as_deref(),
                    Some(baseline.as_str())
                );

                let generated = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap();
                let destination = root.join(generated.suggested_file_name());
                generated.write_new(&destination).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&destination).unwrap(), baseline);
                assert!(generated.write_new(&destination).is_err());

                page.load_quick_into_visual(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick);
                assert!(
                    page.error
                        .as_ref()
                        .is_some_and(|message| message.contains("Redis 高级编辑未接入"))
                );
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn tomcat_quick_bundle_stacks_excludes_removes_resets_copies_and_saves_without_overwrite(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-tomcat-stacking-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.tomcat_version, TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0);
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Tomcat(_))
                ));
                let baseline = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned();
                for expected in [
                    "===== BEGIN conf/server.xml =====",
                    "===== BEGIN webapps/ROOT/META-INF/context.xml =====",
                    "===== BEGIN webapps/ROOT/WEB-INF/web.xml =====",
                    "address=\"127.0.0.1\"",
                    "port=\"8080\"",
                    "connectionTimeout=\"20000\"",
                    "maxThreads=\"200\"",
                    "maxPostSize=\"2097152\"",
                    "web-app_6_1.xsd",
                    "javax.servlet",
                ] {
                    if expected == "javax.servlet" {
                        assert!(!baseline.contains(expected));
                    } else {
                        assert!(baseline.contains(expected), "missing {expected}");
                    }
                }

                for (input, value) in [
                    (&page.tomcat_application_name_input, "Operations ROOT"),
                    (&page.tomcat_port_input, "8181"),
                    (&page.tomcat_connection_timeout_input, "25000"),
                    (&page.tomcat_max_threads_input, "320"),
                    (&page.tomcat_accept_count_input, "400"),
                    (&page.tomcat_max_connections_input, "12000"),
                    (&page.tomcat_max_post_size_input, "3000000"),
                    (&page.tomcat_max_swallow_size_input, "4000000"),
                    (&page.tomcat_compression_min_size_input, "1024"),
                ] {
                    input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
                }
                page.select_tomcat_connector_address(TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv6Loopback, cx);
                page.select_tomcat_version(TomcatVersionFamily::V10_1, cx);
                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                for expected in [
                    "address=\"::1\"",
                    "port=\"8181\"",
                    "connectionTimeout=\"25000\"",
                    "maxThreads=\"320\"",
                    "acceptCount=\"400\"",
                    "maxConnections=\"12000\"",
                    "maxPostSize=\"3000000\"",
                    "maxSwallowSize=\"4000000\"",
                    "compressionMinSize=\"1024\"",
                    "autoDeploy=\"true\"",
                    "web-app_6_0.xsd",
                    "<http-method>GET</http-method>",
                    "<deny-uncovered-http-methods />",
                ] {
                    assert!(stacked.contains(expected), "missing {expected}");
                }

                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .tomcat_features
                        .contains(&TomcatConfigFeature::AllowSafeMethodsOnly)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .tomcat_features
                        .contains(&TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests)
                );
                let deny_all = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(!deny_all.contains("<http-method>GET</http-method>"));
                assert!(
                    deny_all.contains("<web-resource-name>LovelyRes deny all</web-resource-name>")
                );
                assert!(deny_all.contains("<auth-constraint />"));

                page.remove_tomcat_configuration(TomcatConfigFeature::DenyAllRequests, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("LovelyRes deny all")
                );
                page.remove_tomcat_configuration(TomcatConfigFeature::ResponseCompression, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("compressionMinSize")
                );

                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.tomcat_version, TomcatVersionFamily::V11_0);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.tomcat_connector_address,
                    TomcatConnectorAddress::Ipv4Loopback
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.tomcat_features, default_tomcat_features());
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.active_tomcat_configuration,
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Baseline
                );
                assert_eq!(
                    page.tomcat_application_name_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_APPLICATION_NAME
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(), baseline);

                page.copy_current(cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    cx.read_from_clipboard()
                        .and_then(|item| item.text())
                        .as_deref(),
                    Some(baseline.as_str())
                );

                let GeneratedQuickConfig::Tomcat(generated) =
                    page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap()
                else {
                    panic!("expected Tomcat bundle")
                };
                let destination = root.join(generated.suggested_directory_name());
                generated.write_new(&destination).unwrap();
                for artifact in generated.bundle.artifacts() {
                    assert_eq!(
                        fs::read_to_string(destination.join(artifact.relative_path())).unwrap(),
                        artifact.contents()
                    );
                }
                assert!(generated.write_new(&destination).is_err());

                page.load_quick_into_visual(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick);
                assert!(page.error.as_ref().is_some_and(|message| {
                    message.contains("Tomcat 三工件高级语义编辑与导入未接入")
                }));
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn quick_configuration_search_and_product_switches_never_leak_another_preview(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-product-isolation-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Nginx(_))
                ));
                page.quick_preview_scroll
                    .set_offset(point(px(-20.0), px(-120.0)));
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.quick_preview_scroll.offset(), point(px(0.0), px(0.0)));
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Apache(_))
                ));
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Nginx 反向代理", window, cx)
                });
                page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd)
                );
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Apache(_))
                ));

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Apache 禁用 TRACE", window, cx)
                });
                page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx)
                );
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Nginx(_))
                ));

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Nginx 反向代理", window, cx)
                });
                page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql)
                );
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::MySql(_))
                ));

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Nginx 反向代理", window, cx)
                });
                page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis)
                );
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Redis(_))
                ));

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Nginx 反向代理", window, cx)
                });
                page.handle_database_assignment_query_change(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat)
                );
                assert!(matches!(
                    page.model.generated,
                    Some(GeneratedQuickConfig::Tomcat(_))
                ));

                page.model.selected_goal_subject =
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Nginx);
                assert!(page.save_payload().is_none());
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn reset_only_restores_the_selected_product_family(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-reset-isolation-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.mysql_max_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("400", window, cx));
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .mysql_features
                        .contains(&MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections)
                );
                assert_eq!(page.mysql_max_connections_input.read(cx).value(), "400");
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("max_connections = 400")
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.server_name_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("kept.example", window, cx));
                page.regenerate(cx);
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.server_name_input.read(cx).value(), "kept.example");
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("ServerName kept.example")
                );
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn redis_reset_is_isolated_from_mysql_and_web_state(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-redis-reset-isolation-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.redis_max_clients_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("22000", window, cx));
                page.open_redis_configuration(
                    RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.mysql_max_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("400", window, cx));
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.server_name_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("kept.example", window, cx));
                page.regenerate(cx);

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("maxclients 22000")
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.redis_max_clients_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    DEFAULT_REDIS_MAX_CLIENTS
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.mysql_max_connections_input.read(cx).value(), "400");
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .mysql_features
                        .contains(&MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections)
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.server_name_input.read(cx).value(), "kept.example");
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn tomcat_reset_is_isolated_and_other_product_resets_preserve_tomcat_state(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-tomcat-reset-isolation-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.redis_max_clients_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("22000", window, cx));
                page.open_redis_configuration(
                    RedisConfigurationSelection::Feature(RedisConfigFeature::MaxClients),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.mysql_max_connections_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("400", window, cx));
                page.open_mysql_configuration(
                    MySqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(MySqlConfigFeature::MaxConnections),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.server_name_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("kept.example", window, cx));
                page.regenerate(cx);

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.tomcat_application_name_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("Changed ROOT", window, cx));
                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.tomcat_application_name_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    DEFAULT_TOMCAT_APPLICATION_NAME
                );

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.redis_max_clients_input.read(cx).value(), "22000");
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MySql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.mysql_max_connections_input.read(cx).value(), "400");

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.server_name_input.read(cx).value(), "kept.example");

                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.tomcat_application_name_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("Preserved ROOT", window, cx)
                });
                page.open_tomcat_configuration(
                    TomcatConfigurationSelection::Feature(TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Tomcat),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(
                    page.tomcat_application_name_input.read(cx).value(),
                    "Preserved ROOT"
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .tomcat_features
                        .contains(&TomcatConfigFeature::HostAutoDeploy)
                );
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn apache_catalog_renders_inline_editor_and_preview_in_the_three_column_workspace(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-apache-layout-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(800.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.open_apache_configuration(
                    ApacheConfigurationSelection::Feature(ApacheSiteFeature::ClientBodyLimit),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });

        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-apache-configuration-table")
                .is_some()
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-quick-preview-scroll")
                .is_some()
        );
        let row = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-apache-configuration-row-client-body-limit")
            .expect("Apache request body limit row bounds");
        let editor = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-apache-configuration-editor")
            .expect("Apache inline editor bounds");
        assert_eq!(editor.origin.y, row.origin.y + row.size.height);

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn postgresql_and_mariadb_catalogs_render_versions_search_and_inline_editors(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-postgresql-mariadb-layout-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(800.0));

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::PostgreSql),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        for selector in [
            "config-generator-postgresql-version-V14",
            "config-generator-postgresql-version-V15",
            "config-generator-postgresql-version-V16",
            "config-generator-postgresql-version-V17",
            "config-generator-postgresql-version-V18",
        ] {
            assert!(
                cx.debug_bounds(selector).is_some(),
                "{selector} must be explicitly selectable"
            );
        }
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-table")
                .is_some()
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-quick-preview-scroll")
                .is_some()
        );

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.nginx_goal_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("锁等待 deadlock_timeout", window, cx)
                });
                page.open_postgresql_configuration(
                    PostgreSqlConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        PostgreSqlConfigFeature::LockWaitLogging,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let postgresql_row = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-row-lock-wait-logging")
            .expect("filtered PostgreSQL lock-wait row bounds");
        let postgresql_editor = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-editor")
            .expect("PostgreSQL inline editor bounds");
        assert_eq!(
            postgresql_editor.origin.y,
            postgresql_row.origin.y + postgresql_row.size.height
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-postgresql-configuration-row-connection-capacity")
                .is_none(),
            "search must hide unrelated PostgreSQL rows"
        );

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Database(DatabaseConfigProduct::MariaDb),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        for selector in [
            "config-generator-mariadb-version-V10_11",
            "config-generator-mariadb-version-V11_4",
            "config-generator-mariadb-version-V11_8",
            "config-generator-mariadb-version-V12_3",
        ] {
            assert!(
                cx.debug_bounds(selector).is_some(),
                "{selector} must be explicitly selectable"
            );
        }

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.nginx_goal_search_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("日志保留", window, cx));
                page.open_mariadb_configuration(
                    MariaDbConfigurationSelection::Feature(
                        MariaDbConfigFeature::BinaryLogRetention,
                    ),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
            });
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let mariadb_row = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-mariadb-configuration-row-binary-log-retention")
            .expect("filtered MariaDB retention row bounds");
        let mariadb_editor = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-mariadb-configuration-editor")
            .expect("MariaDB inline editor bounds");
        assert_eq!(
            mariadb_editor.origin.y,
            mariadb_row.origin.y + mariadb_row.size.height
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-mariadb-configuration-row-maximum-packet-size")
                .is_none(),
            "search must hide unrelated MariaDB rows"
        );

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_spa_fallback_swaps_with_static_404_and_removal_restores_baseline(
        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
    ) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-spa-fallback-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_kind(NginxSiteKind::Static, window, cx);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles)
                );

                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback)
                );
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles)
                );
                let spa = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(spa.contains("try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;"));
                assert_eq!(spa.matches("try_files $uri $uri/ =404;").count(), 1);
                assert_eq!(spa.matches("location /assets/ {").count(), 1);

                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("try_files $uri $uri/ =404;")
                );

                page.open_nginx_configuration(
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback),
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                page.remove_nginx_configuration(NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback, cx);
                assert!(
                    !page
                        .model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::SpaFallback)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .nginx_features
                        .contains(&NginxSiteFeature::StaticTryFiles)
                );
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("try_files $uri $uri/ =404;")
                );
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_reset_restores_every_checked_in_default(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-reset-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        let (initial_features, initial_contents) = page.update(cx, |page, _| {
            (
                page.model.nginx_features.clone(),
                page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned(),
            )
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                for (input, value) in [
                    (&page.server_name_input, "changed.example"),
                    (&page.listen_port_input, "8088"),
                    (&page.static_root_input, "/srv/changed"),
                    (&page.upstream_input, "https://127.0.0.1:9443"),
                    (&page.forbidden_path_input, "/private"),
                    (&page.allowed_source_input, "192.0.2.0/24"),
                    (&page.nginx_goal_search_input, "gzip"),
                ] {
                    input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value(value, window, cx));
                }
                page.model.kind = NginxSiteKind::Static;
                page.model.nginx_features = BTreeSet::from([
                    NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403,
                    NginxSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp,
                ]);
                page.model.active_nginx_configuration =
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Feature(NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403);
                page.model.forbidden_path_scope = NginxForbiddenPathScope::Exact;
                page.error = Some("stale error".into());
                page.notice = Some("stale notice".into());
                page.quick_preview_scroll
                    .set_offset(point(px(-40.0), px(-80.0)));

                page.saving = true;
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.kind, NginxSiteKind::Static);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.nginx_goal_search_input.read(cx).value().as_ref(),
                    "gzip"
                );

                page.saving = false;
                page.reset_goal_catalog_search(window, cx);

                assert_eq!(page.model.kind, NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
                assert_eq!(page.model.nginx_features, initial_features);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.active_nginx_configuration,
                    NginxConfigurationSelection::Baseline
                );
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.forbidden_path_scope,
                    NginxForbiddenPathScope::PathAndDescendants
                );
                for (input, expected) in [
                    (&page.server_name_input, DEFAULT_SERVER_NAME),
                    (&page.listen_port_input, DEFAULT_LISTEN_PORT),
                    (&page.static_root_input, DEFAULT_STATIC_ROOT),
                    (&page.upstream_input, DEFAULT_UPSTREAM),
                    (&page.forbidden_path_input, DEFAULT_FORBIDDEN_PATH),
                    (&page.allowed_source_input, DEFAULT_ALLOWED_SOURCE),
                    (&page.nginx_goal_search_input, ""),
                ] {
                    assert_eq!(input.read(cx).value().as_ref(), expected);
                }
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents(),
                    initial_contents
                );
                assert_eq!(page.quick_preview_scroll.offset(), point(px(0.0), px(0.0)));
                assert!(page.error.is_none());
                assert!(page.notice.is_none());
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_quick_preview_scrolls_independently_through_long_output(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-preview-scroll-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.allowed_source_input
                    .update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("10.0.0.0/8", window, cx));
                page.model.nginx_features = BTreeSet::from([
                    NginxSiteFeature::ForbiddenPath403,
                    NginxSiteFeature::SecurityHeaders,
                    NginxSiteFeature::DisableDirectoryListing,
                    NginxSiteFeature::RestrictRequestMethods,
                    NginxSiteFeature::HideVersion,
                    NginxSiteFeature::RestrictSourceIp,
                    NginxSiteFeature::GzipCompression,
                    NginxSiteFeature::StaticAssetCaching,
                    NginxSiteFeature::WebSocketProxy,
                ]);
                page.regenerate(cx);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .lines()
                        .count()
                        > 50
                );
            });
        });

        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(800.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let preview_bounds = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-quick-preview-scroll")
            .expect("stable right preview scroll viewport bounds");
        let preview_scroll = page.update(cx, |page, _| page.quick_preview_scroll.clone());
        let page_scroll = page.update(cx, |page, _| page.page_scroll.clone());
        assert!(
            preview_scroll.max_offset().height > px(0.0),
            "long generated output must overflow inside the right preview"
        );

        cx.simulate_event(ScrollWheelEvent {
            position: preview_bounds.center(),
            delta: ScrollDelta::Pixels(point(px(0.0), px(-5_000.0))),
            ..Default::default()
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        assert_eq!(
            preview_scroll.offset().y,
            -preview_scroll.max_offset().height,
            "wheel input over the preview must reach its final configuration line"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            page_scroll.offset().y,
            px(0.0),
            "preview scrolling must not move the configuration page"
        );

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    fn redis_quick_preview_scrolls_to_the_last_generated_directive(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(

            Uuid::new_v4()

        });
        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.select_goal_subject(
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::Redis),
                    window,

                );
                page.model.redis_features = RedisConfigFeature::ALL.into_iter().collect();
                page.model.redis_eviction_policy = RedisEvictionPolicy::AllKeysLru;
                page.model.redis_append_fsync = RedisAppendFsync::EverySecond;

        let page_scroll = page.update(cx, |page, _| page.page_scroll.clone());
        assert!(preview_scroll.max_offset().height > px(0.0));

        cx.simulate_event(ScrollWheelEvent {
            position: preview_bounds.center(),
            delta: ScrollDelta::Pixels(point(px(0.0), px(-5_000.0))),
            ..Default::default()

            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())

        assert_eq!(
            preview_scroll.offset().y,
            -preview_scroll.max_offset().height
        );
        assert_eq!(page_scroll.offset().y, px(0.0));

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[test]
    fn visual_workspace_layout_keeps_small_windows_scrollable_and_panels_bounded() {
        let layout = VISUAL_WORKSPACE_LAYOUT;
        assert!(layout.split_min_height > 480.0);
        assert!(
            layout.editor_max_height + layout.preview_min_height + 24.0 <= layout.split_min_height
        );
        assert!(layout.node_row_height >= 56.0);

        let long_text = "可读摘要".repeat(80);
        let label = bounded_visual_row_text(&long_text, MAX_VISUAL_ROW_LABEL_CHARS);
        let preview = bounded_visual_row_text(&long_text, MAX_VISUAL_ROW_PREVIEW_CHARS);
        assert_eq!(label.chars().count(), MAX_VISUAL_ROW_LABEL_CHARS + 1);
        assert_eq!(preview.chars().count(), MAX_VISUAL_ROW_PREVIEW_CHARS + 1);
        assert!(label.ends_with('…'));
        assert!(preview.ends_with('…'));
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn short_viewport_scrolls_page_from_header_to_full_preview(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-scroll-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| {
                let mut page = ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx);
                page.model.mode = ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual;
                page
            })
        });

        let short_viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(420.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), short_viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let page_scroll = page.update(cx, |page, _| page.page_scroll.clone());
        assert!(
            page_scroll.max_offset().height > px(0.0),
            "the complete visual workspace must overflow the real app-shell mount"
        );
        let initial_preview = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-full-preview")
            .expect("full preview bounds after initial draw");
        assert!(
            initial_preview.origin.y >= short_viewport.height,
            "the short viewport must require page scrolling to reach the full preview"
        );

        cx.simulate_event(ScrollWheelEvent {
            position: point(px(1_100.0), px(24.0)),
            delta: ScrollDelta::Pixels(point(px(0.0), px(-5_000.0))),
            ..Default::default()
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), short_viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        assert_eq!(
            page_scroll.offset().y,
            -page_scroll.max_offset().height,
            "a wheel gesture on the fixed page header must reach the page bottom"
        );
        let preview = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-full-preview")
            .expect("full preview bounds after bottom scroll");
        let preview_bottom = preview.origin.y + preview.size.height;
        assert!(
            preview.origin.y < short_viewport.height && preview_bottom > px(0.0),
            "the complete preview must intersect the viewport at the page bottom"
        );
        assert!(
            preview_bottom <= short_viewport.height,
            "the complete preview bottom must be reachable, got {preview_bottom:?}"
        );

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_403_goal_is_discoverable_and_drives_the_typed_preview(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "lovelyres-config-generator-goal-{}",
            Uuid::new_v4()
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let services =
            Arc::new(BackendServices::new(PortableStore::prepare(root.clone()).unwrap()).unwrap());
        let tokio = TokioBridge::new().unwrap();

        cx.update(gpui_component::init);
        let cx = cx.add_empty_window();
        let page = cx.update(|window, cx| {
            let shared = cx.new(|_| SharedAppState::default());
            cx.new(|cx| ConfigGeneratorPage::new(services, tokio, shared, window, cx))
        });
        let viewport = size(px(1_200.0), px(900.0));
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-row-forbidden-path-403")
                .is_some(),
            "the reviewed 403 configuration must be visible in the flat table"
        );
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-table-header")
                .is_some(),
            "the Nginx configuration catalog must use a stable table header"
        );

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.start_curated_configuration_goal(
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxStaticSite,
                    "",
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.kind, NginxSiteKind::Static);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("root \"/var/www/html\";")
                );
                page.start_curated_configuration_goal(
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::NginxReverseProxy,
                    "",
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.kind, NginxSiteKind::ReverseProxy);
                assert!(
                    page.model
                        .generated
                        .as_ref()
                        .unwrap()
                        .contents()
                        .contains("proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3000;")
                );
            });
        });

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.start_forbidden_path_goal("让 /admin 从外部访问全部返回 403", window, cx);
                cx.focus_handle().focus(window);
            });
        });
        let generated = page.update(cx, |page, _| {
            page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents().to_owned()
        });
        assert!(generated.contains("location = /admin"));
        assert!(generated.contains("location ^~ /admin/"));

        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        let forbidden_row = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-row-forbidden-path-403")
            .expect("active 403 row bounds");
        let inline_editor = cx
            .debug_bounds("config-generator-configuration-editor")
            .expect("inline 403 editor bounds");
        assert_eq!(
            inline_editor.origin.y,
            forbidden_row.origin.y + forbidden_row.size.height,
            "the 403 parameters must render immediately below the selected 403 row"
        );

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.load_quick_into_visual(window, cx);
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Visual);
                assert!(!page.model.visual_dirty);
                assert_eq!(page.model.visual_document.raw_bytes(), generated.as_bytes());

                let locations = page
                    .model
                    .visual_rows
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|row| row.name() == Some("location"))
                    .filter(|row| matches!(row.arguments(), Some("= /admin" | "^~ /admin/")))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>();
                assert_eq!(locations.len(), 2);
                for location in locations {
                    assert!(page.model.visual_rows.iter().any(|row| {
                        row.parent == Some(location.id())
                            && row.name() == Some("return")
                            && row.arguments() == Some("403")
                    }));
                }

                page.visual_search_input.update(cx, |input, cx| {
                    input.set_value("/admin 外部访问返回 403", window, cx)
                });
                cx.notify();
            });
        });
        cx.draw(point(px(0.0), px(0.0)), viewport, |_, _| {
            app_shell_scroll_mount(page.clone())
        });
        assert!(
            cx.debug_bounds("config-generator-visual-forbidden-goal-result")
                .is_some(),
            "the existing document search must surface the matching goal recipe"
        );

        cx.update(|window, cx| {
            page.update(cx, |page, cx| {
                page.start_curated_configuration_goal(
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheForbiddenPath403,
                    "Apache block /admin.v1 with 403",
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                assert_eq!(page.model.mode, ConfigGeneratorMode::Quick);
                assert_eq!(
                    page.model.selected_goal_subject,
                    ConfigurationGoalSubject::Middleware(MiddlewareProduct::ApacheHttpd)
                );
                let forbidden = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(forbidden.contains("<VirtualHost *:80>"));
                assert!(forbidden.contains("^/admin\\.v1(?:/.*)?$"));
                assert!(forbidden.contains("Require all denied"));
                assert!(forbidden.contains("ProxyPass"));

                page.start_curated_configuration_goal(
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheDisableTrace,
                    "Apache 禁用 TRACE；Header set X-Evil yes",
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let trace = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(trace.contains("TraceEnable Off"));
                assert!(trace.contains("^/admin\\.v1(?:/.*)?$"));
                assert!(trace.contains("ProxyPass"));

                page.start_curated_configuration_goal(
                    ConfigurationGoalRecipeAction::ApacheReduceVersionDisclosure,
                    "Apache 隐藏版本号",
                    window,
                    cx,
                );
                let stacked = page.model.generated.as_ref().unwrap().contents();
                assert!(stacked.contains("ServerTokens Prod"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("ServerSignature Off"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("TraceEnable Off"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("^/admin\\.v1(?:/.*)?$"));
                assert!(stacked.contains("ProxyPass"));
            });
        });

        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[gpui::test]
    fn nginx_import_result_replaces_a_stale_apache_visual_document(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
